### [57] [Conditional Visual Evidence Utility: State-Dependent Rank Reversals in Frozen Vision-Language Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28316)
*Yunxuan Fang,Xinhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型中证据重要性是否具有状态依赖性，即在观察到某些线索后，剩余证据的价值排序会发生变化。通过在受控组合视觉搜索任务中验证OpenCLIP和SigLIP模型，发现存在稳健的状态依赖排名反转，且基于首次获取后重排的证据选择策略在跨评估者场景中能保留决策价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的静态重要性分数将视觉证据压缩为单一排序，但忽略了证据在部分观测后的条件价值可能发生改变。作者质疑静态排序是否足以描述视觉-语言模型中的证据使用方式，并提出应当以条件性的视角来评估和指导证据选择。

Method: 在受控组合视觉搜索任务中独立暴露颜色、形状和纹理证据，测量不同获取状态下的条件边际效用。使用OpenCLIP和SigLIP模型在800个场景上进行验证，评估状态依赖的排名反转，并通过查询-场景失排（derangement）作为对照。此外进行了探索性的匹配首次动作分析，评估在一种配置下训练、在另一种配置下评估的重排策略的决策效用。

Result: OpenCLIP和SigLIP均展现出稳健的状态依赖排名反转，这些反转集中在候选重叠区域。该结构在两种证据累积构造和十种等价查询措辞下持续存在，但在查询-场景失排条件下消失。重排策略在跨证据模式、措辞或骨干网络的评估中产生正的第二步效用。

Conclusion: 证据重要性在受控设置下具有状态依赖性，更新证据排序可以在评估者变化时保留决策相关价值。研究建议应以条件性方式评估视觉-语言模型中的证据使用，而非依赖单一静态排名，并为未来自适应证据选择方法提供了可测量的目标。

Abstract: Static importance scores compress visual evidence into a single ranking, but the value of remaining evidence can change after one cue has been observed. We study this possibility in controlled compositional visual search, where color, shape, and texture evidence can be independently exposed and their conditional marginal utility measured across acquisition states. In a held-out confirmation on 800 scenes, frozen OpenCLIP and SigLIP exhibit robust state-dependent rank reversals that concentrate in candidate-overlap regimes designed to induce ordering changes. The structure persists across two evidence-accumulation constructions and ten equivalent query wordings, but disappears under query-scene derangement. We also ask whether these reversals matter for decisions. In a post-confirmation exploratory matched-first-action analysis, reranking only after the first acquisition yields positive step-2 utility when decisions are selected under one evidence mode, wording, or backbone and evaluated under another. Together, these results show that evidence importance is state-dependent in this controlled setup and that updating an evidence ordering can retain decision-relevant value across evaluator changes. They motivate evaluating vision-language evidence use conditionally rather than through a single static ranking, while providing a measurable target for future adaptive evidence-selection methods.

</details>


### [58] [Abstract4D: A Large-Scale Dataset and Framework for Understanding the Visual Language of Abstract Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28339)
*Haowei Zhang,Yuanpei Zhao,Ji-Zhe Zhou,Mao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Abstract4D，迄今最大的抽象绘画数据集（超过12万张图像），配有丰富的元数据和多维提示词（形式、色彩、纹理、构图）。通过人机混合标注流程生成注释，并在此基础上进行语义结构分析和基准任务评估，探索AI如何感知和再现抽象视觉语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI能够分类艺术风格和合成图像，但缺乏对赋予艺术意义的视觉语言的理解。抽象绘画最小化对象语义、突出结构线索，是检验计算感知的理想测试平台，但目前缺乏大规模、多维标注的抽象绘画数据集。

Method: 构建Abstract4D数据集：包含超过120,000张抽象绘画图像，通过人类与VLM混合管线生成高质量一致性注释，覆盖形式、色彩、纹理、构图四个感知维度；并利用该数据集进行嵌入可视化分析和三类基准任务（分类、跨模态检索、文生图）评估。

Result: 通过大规模嵌入可视化揭示了抽象艺术的语义结构和感知关系组织方式；建立的基准任务可用于评估AI模型在分类、跨模态检索和文本到图像生成任务中感知与再现抽象视觉语言的能力。

Conclusion: Abstract4D为AI理解和解释抽象艺术提供了大规模数据基础，既支持探索性分析，也支持定量评估，弥补了AI在抽象视觉语言理解方面的空白。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence can classify artistic styles and synthesize images, but it still lacks a model of the visual language that gives art meaning. Abstract painting minimizes object semantics and foregrounds structural cues, making it an ideal testbed for computational perception. We introduce \textbf{Abstract4D}, the largest dataset of abstract paintings to date: more than 120,000 images paired with rich metadata and multi-dimensional prompts that capture each work's perceptual attributes---\textit{form, color, texture, and composition}. Annotations are produced by a hybrid human--VLM pipeline for quality and consistency. Using Abstract4D, we (i) analyze the semantic structure of abstract art through large-scale embedding visualization, uncovering how perceptual relationships organize artistic meaning, and (ii) establish benchmark tasks for classification, cross-modal retrieval, and text-to-image generation to evaluate how AI models perceive and reproduce abstract visual language. Together, these analyses demonstrate how Abstract4D enables both exploration and quantitative assessment of AI's ability to represent and interpret abstract art.

</details>


### [59] [Cross-Spectral Dense Correspondence for Multimodal Spectral Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28341)
*Eric L. Wisotzky,Jost Triller,Simon W. Härtl,Oliver T. Bruns,Peter Eisert,Anna Hilsmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种传感器无关的跨光谱调制协议和合成跨光谱对应基准，用于解决多光谱成像系统中密集对应匹配的监督数据不足问题，在多种现代匹配骨干网络上显著提升了严重光谱失配下的匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光谱成像系统中不同波长范围的融合需要精确的密集对应关系，但不同光谱通道之间存在对比度变化、强度反转和外观偏移等问题，且难以获取密集真值，传统RGB训练数据的监督能力有限。

Method: 提出传感器无关的跨光谱调制协议（包含强度输入投影），并在已有对应基准上构建合成跨光谱对应基准，模拟物理上合理的辐射度差异；通过统一跨光谱协议训练多种现代密集对应骨干网络。

Result: 在严重光谱失配下模型性能大幅提升，同时保持标准RGB基准上的性能；消融实验表明视角相关通道选择和非线性辐射度变换提供互补的鲁棒性，现有模型的主要瓶颈是训练分布与目标光谱特征不匹配而非结构匹配能力不足。

Conclusion: 该训练数据增强协议可作为异构医学光谱采集系统中空间相干光谱融合的关键使能技术，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Precise dense correspondence is a fundamental prerequisite for multimodal spectral imaging systems that fuse disparate wavelength ranges for subsequent analysis in medical and scientific imaging. Corresponding image points are often observed with non-overlapping spectral sensitivities, leading to wavelength-dependent contrast changes, intensity inversions, and appearance shifts for which dense ground truth is difficult to obtain and conventional RGB-based training data provides only limited supervision. We address this data gap by introducing a sensor-agnostic cross-spectral modulation protocol on established correspondence benchmarks with intensity input projection, and by proposing a synthetic cross-spectral correspondence benchmark simulating physically plausible radiometric differences. Evaluation on several modern dense correspondence backbones trained with our unified cross-spectral protocol showed substantial improvements under severe spectral mismatch while maintaining performance on standard RGB benchmarks. Ablation experiments show that view-dependent channel selection and nonlinear radiometric transformations provide complementary robustness, indicating that the primary limitation of existing models is not their structural matching capacity but the mismatch between training distribution and spectral characteristics of the target image pair. Qualitative evaluations on heterogeneous medical spectral acquisition systems demonstrate the practical relevance of the proposed training data augmentation protocol as an enabler for spatially coherent spectral fusion in HSI workflows.

</details>


### [60] [Denoising-Aware Temporal Point Cloud Completion for 3D Crop Architecture Recovery and Phenotypic Trait Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28343)
*Mrudul Mittal,Soumyashree Kar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SynthCrop4D，一个程序化生成的时序植物点云合成数据集，并提供完整的几何真值用于基准测试。同时提出一个两阶段流水线（空间去噪+时序点云补全），在合成和真实数据上验证了去噪对重建质量的提升，并展示了补全点云在植物表型特征提取中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量表型分析依赖于植物跨生长阶段的精确3D重建，但缺乏带有完整几何真值的时序补全数据集，限制了时序补全方法的开发和评估。

Method: 1) 构建SynthCrop4D合成数据集，提供可控噪声、遮挡和完整植物几何。2) 提出两阶段流水线：先通过去噪模块移除激光扫描点云的结构伪影，再用Adaptive Temporal PoinTr模型利用前一时刻(t-1)信息重建当前时刻(t)的生长阶段，恢复因自遮挡缺失的区域。

Result: 去噪显著提升重建质量，最佳配置在SynthCrop4D上达到Chamfer Distance 0.0061（Temporal PoinTr + Mamba-DG），在Pheno4D上达到F-Score 0.2080（Vanilla PoinTr + Mamba-DG）。补全点云用于提取株高、冠幅和凸包体积等表型特征，凸包体积MAE在合成数据上为0.021，真实数据上为0.343。

Conclusion: SynthCrop4D和所提出的流水线为时序植物重建和高通量作物表型分析提供了基准和方法论。

Abstract: High-throughput phenotyping depends on accurate 3D reconstruction of plants across growth stages, yet the development and evaluation of temporal completion methods are limited by the lack of datasets with complete geometric ground truth. To address this challenge, we introduce SynthCrop4D, a procedurally generated synthetic dataset of temporally evolving plant point clouds that provides controllable noise, occlusion, and complete plant geometry for benchmarking reconstruction methods. Using this dataset, we evaluate a two-stage pipeline that combines spatial denoising and temporal point cloud completion. First, a denoising module removes structural artifacts from raw laser-scanned point clouds. The resulting data are then processed by an Adaptive Temporal PoinTr model that reconstructs the current growth stage (t) using information from the previous stage (t-1), enabling recovery of regions missing due to self-occlusion. We evaluate the proposed framework on both SynthCrop4D and the real-world Pheno4D dataset (tomato and maize) under settings with and without denoising. Results show that denoising substantially improves reconstruction quality, with the best configuration achieving a Chamfer Distance of 0.0061 on SynthCrop4D (Temporal PoinTr + Mamba-DG) and an F-Score of 0.2080 on Pheno4D (Vanilla PoinTr + Mamba-DG). We further demonstrate the use of completed point clouds for phenotypic trait extraction, including plant height, canopy width, and convex hull volume, obtaining hull-volume MAEs of 0.021 on synthetic data and 0.343 on real data. Together, SynthCrop4D and the proposed pipeline provide a benchmark and methodology for temporal plant reconstruction and high-throughput crop phenotyping.

</details>


### [61] [Real-Time Musculoskeletal Surrogates for Pediatric Cerebral Palsy: a Credibility Pilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28371)
*Mohammad Arif Ul Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种面向脑瘫儿童的对象条件因果神经代理模型，用于实时重建肌肉骨骼参数，在留一受试者验证中实现R²=0.92-0.95的肌肉腱长度预测精度，推理延迟低于100毫秒目标，并揭示了当前模型在力学预测不确定性校准方面的严重缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时肌肉骨骼代理模型有望为脑瘫儿童提供个性化康复支持，但其可信度取决于逐对象评估、低推理延迟和校准不确定性。现有方法在这三个方面均存在不足。

Method: 构建对象条件因果神经代理模型，融合OpenSim静态参数、时间关节运动学、真实肌肉容量和训练期扰动；采用留一对象验证（6个开发对象），并在3个锁定测试对象上评估冻结配置；使用蒙特卡洛方法进行可信度试点测试。

Result: 肌肉腱长度预测R²=0.92（开发验证）和约0.95（锁定对象），nRMSE<8%；推理延迟仅亚毫秒至几毫秒，远低于100毫秒交互康复目标；但肌肉力估计不稳定，且±5%的测量扰动导致名义90%置信区间实际覆盖率极低（力约4%，MT长度低于1%）。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了儿科肌肉骨骼代理模型的无泄漏评估和可信度框架，同时指出力学建模和认知不确定性是未来临床可信数字孪生的核心挑战。

Abstract: Real-time musculoskeletal (MSK) surrogates could support personalized rehabilitation for children with cerebral palsy (CP), but their credibility depends on subject-wise evaluation, low inference latency, and calibrated uncertainty. We develop a subject-conditioned causal neural surrogate using OpenSim-derived static parameters, temporal joint kinematics, true muscle capacities, and training-only perturbations. On a real pediatric CP gait dataset comprising nine children, we use leave-one-subject-out validation on six development subjects and evaluate a frozen configuration once on three locked test subjects. The surrogate accurately reproduces musculotendon lengths (R-square = 0.92 in development validation and approximately 0.95 on locked subjects; nRMSE < 8%) while requiring only sub-millisecond to few-millisecond neural inference, well below a 100 ms interactive-rehabilitation target. In contrast, direct muscle-force estimation remains unstable at this small, heterogeneous scale: pooled metrics can overstate within-subject, per-muscle accuracy. A Monte Carlo credibility pilot further shows that propagating only +/-5% anthropometry and muscle-capacity variation produces severely overconfident nominal 90% intervals (approximately 4% force coverage and below 1% MT-length coverage). These results establish a leakage-free evaluation and credibility framework for pediatric MSK surrogates, while identifying force modeling and epistemic uncertainty as the central next challenges for clinically credible digital twins.

</details>


### [62] [Semantic Head Specialization Guides Hybrid ViT Attention for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28383)
*Chenhong He,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Hanglong Lv,Lingpeng Kong,Qi Liu,Tong Yang,Shuhuai Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多模态大语言模型中视觉Transformer（ViT）的注意力机制，发现注意力头会分化为"对象专家"和"背景专家"两种角色，并提出SHS-Index量化这种专门化程度，进而设计出名为Ariadne Attention的混合注意力机制，在22个图像和视频任务上以6.5倍更少的注意力计算达到与全注意力相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合注意力主导了前沿LLM，但多模态LLM中的ViT缺乏令人满意的混合设计，且对于为什么某些注意力模式效果更好尚无共识。作者希望填补这一研究空白，探究ViT注意力头的分化规律，并为设计原则性的混合注意力提供依据。

Method: 作者研究ViT注意力头，发现它们分化为对象专家和背景专家角色（称为语义头专门化SHS），并提出了SHS-Index来量化这种专门化。通过SHS-Index区分全注意力和分块窗口ViT，并识别出三个影响SHS的结构因素：窗口交互、令牌序列化和局部softmax分配。基于这些因素设计混合注意力Ariadne Attention。

Result: SHS-Index能够区分全注意力和分块窗口ViT，且与下游基准性能强相关。Ariadne Attention在22个图像和视频任务上与全注意力性能相匹配，同时注意力计算减少6.5倍。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了注意力头专门化作为一种可测量的属性，可用于诊断和设计原则性的混合ViT注意力机制，为多模态LLM规模的注意力设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Hybrid attention dominates frontier LLMs, yet Vision Transformers (ViTs) in multimodal LLMs lack a satisfactory hybrid design, with no consensus on why certain attention patterns work better. To fill this gap, we study ViT attention heads and find they differentiate into object- and background-specialist roles, a pattern most pronounced under full attention; we call this Semantic Head Specialization (SHS). We propose SHS-Index to quantify this specialization, show that it distinguishes full-attention from chunk-window ViTs, and find that it strongly tracks downstream benchmark performance. We then identify three structural factors that shape SHS---window interaction, token serialization, and local softmax allocation---and use them as design principles for hybrid attention. Guided by these factors, we design Ariadne Attention, a hybrid that matches full attention on 22 image and video tasks at 6.5x less attention compute. Our findings establish head specialization as a measurable property for diagnosing and designing principled hybrid ViT attention at the multimodal-LLM scale.

</details>


### [63] [GraspHOI: Full-Body 3D Human-Object Reconstruction with Finger-Level Grasps from a Single In-the-Wild Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28386)
*Semin Kim,Haechan Shin,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraspHOI是首个从单张图像重建全身3D人-物交互的框架，显式优化手指关节与重建物体对齐，实现了无预定义网格、无固定类别词汇的类别无关物体重建，并通过遮挡感知掌部对应和接触感知优化提升交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目全身3D人-物交互方法未能将显式手指级抓取优化与类别无关物体重建相结合，导致手指悬浮或穿透物体而非形成真实抓握姿态。

Method: 分别重建身体、手和物体，通过基于深度的注册和图像空间对齐在度量相机空间中对齐；利用遮挡感知掌部对应将物体与抓握手定位，接触感知优化细化手臂和手指关节以形成表面接触并避免过度穿透。

Result: 在四个基准测试和六个基线上，GraspHOI在相对人-物放置、手部精度和接触合理性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: GraspHOI通过显式手指级抓取优化与类别无关物体重建的结合，有效解决了手指漂浮/穿透问题，显著提升了单目全身人-物交互重建的真实感和精度。

Abstract: Existing monocular full-body 3D human-object interaction (HOI) methods do not combine explicit finger-level grasp optimization with category-agnostic object reconstruction. Despite plausible body-object configurations, their fingers may float from or penetrate objects instead of forming a grasp. We present GraspHOI, the first framework that reconstructs a full-body 3D HOI from a single image while explicitly optimizing finger articulation against the reconstructed object. GraspHOI recovers object geometry directly, without predefined meshes or a fixed category vocabulary. It reconstructs the body, hands, and object separately, aligning them in metric camera space via depth-based registration and image-space alignment. Occlusion-aware palmar correspondences seat the object against the grasping hand, and contact-aware optimization refines arm and finger articulation to form surface contact without excessive penetration. Across four benchmarks and six baselines, GraspHOI improves relative human-object placement, hand accuracy, and contact plausibility. Full pipeline code will be released.

</details>


### [64] [How Far Can 5,500 Hours of Driving Take You? A Scaling Law Analysis of Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28404)
*Victor Besnier,Anh-Quan Cao,Elias Ramzi,Spyros Gidaris,Tuan-Hung Vu,Andrei Bursuc,Eloi Zablocki,Matthieu Cord*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对自动驾驶视频扩散模型进行了系统性的缩放定律研究，从1M到9B参数规模的模型在不同训练暴露量下进行实验，发现验证损失遵循幂定律，并据此训练了9B参数的最大开源自动驾驶视频生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶视频生成无法像网页数据那样随意获取数据，驾驶数据采集成本高且受隐私限制，因此需要充分利用固定语料库。研究者希望通过缩放定律研究来回答训练预算分配问题：应该延长训练时间还是增大模型规模，以及是否需要更多数据。

Method: 研究者从零开始在驾驶数据上训练了一系列视频扩散模型（1M到9B参数），在不同训练暴露量下进行系统实验，建立了损失与模型大小及训练暴露量之间的幂定律关系，并据此指导训练一个9B参数的大型模型。

Result: 验证损失在模型大小和训练暴露量上均遵循一致的幂定律。训练暴露量对损失的改善速度远快于模型大小，因此在有限计算下延长训练是最有效的方法。但更大模型能达到更低渐近损失，当计算和数据充足时仍应增大模型。最终训练的9B参数模型成为自动驾驶驾驶视频生成的开源SOTA。

Conclusion: 该研究为自动驾驶视频生成提供了系统的缩放定律指导，证明了在数据受限场景下延长训练时间优于增大模型规模，同时在资源充足时增大模型仍具优势，并为该领域确立了新的开源基准。

Abstract: Video generation for autonomous driving cannot follow the web-scale route: driving data is expensive to collect, bound by privacy requirements, and cannot be scraped at will, so models must make the most of a fixed corpus. We present a systematic scaling-law study of video diffusion models trained from scratch on driving data: a family of models from 1M to 9B parameters, trained at different exposures on up to 5,500 hours of driving. Validation loss follows consistent power laws in both model size and training exposure, answering the questions that shape a training budget: whether compute is better spent on longer training or on a larger model, and whether more data is needed. Loss improves much faster with training exposure than with model size, making longer training the most effective way to improve a fixed model under limited compute. However, larger models continue to achieve lower asymptotic loss, so compute-optimal scaling still favors increasing model size when sufficient compute and data are available. Guided by these laws, we train a 9B-parameter model, to our knowledge the largest video diffusion model trained from scratch on driving data: it sets a new open-source state of the art for driving video generation, as measured on nuScenes. Our code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/valeoai/VATIX. NATIX is separately releasing the underlying driving data in stages.

</details>


### [65] [Post-Training VLMs for Video Mistake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28406)
*Federico Spurio,Olga Zatsarynna,Lars Doorenbos,Emad Bahrami,Gianpiero Francesca,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mistake Detection Video Question Answering (MD-VQA)协议和基准，用于检测视频中执行步骤是否与指令描述一致，覆盖已见和未见动作；并提出首个针对错误检测的视频语言模型后训练技术，通过定制奖励函数促使模型识别指令与视频之间的差异，在公开基准上超越多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频错误检测方法主要基于封闭集协议，任何任务变化都需要重新收集数据和训练模型，限制了其广泛适用性。作者认为错误检测方法应学习错误的一般概念，而非过拟合特定步骤细节。

Method: 1) 提出MD-VQA协议和配套基准，测试方法能否根据描述判断步骤执行是否正确（覆盖已见和未见动作）；2) 提出首个视频语言模型后训练技术用于错误检测，采用定制奖励函数鼓励模型识别指令与视频之间的差异。

Result: 该方法在多个评估中优于零样本、监督微调和后训练基线；在未见流程上泛化尤其出色，例如在EP-VQA上相比最佳基线提升高达11.6%。

Conclusion: 本文验证了学习错误的一般概念而非过拟合步骤细节的有效性，为通用错误检测铺平了道路，并公开发布代码和基准。

Abstract: Human mistakes are inevitable when following instructions, yet they can lead to severe consequences. As such, there has been an increased interest in developing methods for detecting mistakes in videos, with current methods mostly focusing on closed-set protocols. While successful in controlled settings, the closed-set assumption limits their wider applicability, as any changes to the task require collecting new data and re-training models. Instead, we argue that mistake detection methods should learn the general concept of a mistake, rather than overfitting to step-specific details. To reflect this, we introduce the Mistake Detection Video Question Answering (MD-VQA) protocol and accompanying benchmark. MD-VQA tests whether methods can discern if a step was executed correctly with respect to its description, for both seen and unseen actions. To address this important challenge, we propose the first video-language-model post-training technique for mistake detection. Our method uses a tailored reward function to encourage the model to identify discrepancies between an instruction and the corresponding video. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that this approach outperforms zero-shot, supervised fine-tuning, and post-training baselines. Notably, our method generalizes especially well to unseen procedures, for instance, with an improvement of up to 11.6% over the best-performing baseline on EP-VQA, paving the way toward general mistake detection. We release our code and benchmark at https://github.com/FedeSpu/mstk.

</details>


### [66] [Lossy Event Compression: From Event Stream Distortion to Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28429)
*Zahra Rezaee,Catarina Brites,João Ascenso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种事件相机压缩流水线（基于聚合的直方图文方法JPEG 2000和基于点云的G-PCC方法），并在统一的任务驱动评估框架下首次将五种分类型失真度量应用于事件压缩，实验证明这些度量能可靠预测压缩导致的任务性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在高速运动场景下产生大量事件数据，造成带宽和存储挑战。现有事件流失真度量无法在任务层面可靠预测压缩退化，导致编码优化依赖昂贵的任务级评估。

Method: 提出两条压缩流水线：1）基于聚合的方法将事件流转换为极性直方图文并通过JPEG 2000压缩；2）基于无帧点云的方法使用G-PCC八叉树编码器原生编码事件为3D点。在统一任务驱动评估框架下，涵盖视频重建、目标检测、光流估计和异步特征跟踪四个任务，首次将五种分类型失真度量应用于事件压缩并与现有度量进行基准对比。

Result: 实验表明所提出的失真度量能在不同编码框架下可靠预测压缩导致的任务退化，说明事件流失真评估可以替代重复的任务级评估。

Conclusion: 事件流失真度量可作为任务级评估的高效替代方案，为未来事件数据编码方案的开发和优化提供直接指导。

Abstract: Event cameras generate asynchronous, sparse data streams with microsecond temporal resolution, but in moderate-to-high motion scenes they can produce as many as hundreds of millions of events per second, creating significant bandwidth and storage challenges. Lossy compression is therefore essential for practical deployment, yet existing event stream distortion metrics fail to reliably predict compression-induced degradation at the task level, forcing codec optimization to rely on expensive task-specific evaluations. To address this gap, this paper introduces two fundamentally different event compression pipelines: i) an aggregation-based pipeline that converts the event stream into polarity-based histogram frames for compression with the conventional image codec JPEG 2000, and ii) a frame-free point cloud-based pipeline that codes events natively as 3D points using the octree-based codec G-PCC. Both pipelines are then assessed within a unified task-driven evaluation framework that relates event stream distortion to downstream application performance across four representative tasks: i) video reconstruction, ii) object detection, iii) optical flow estimation, and a delay-sensitive task iv) asynchronous feature tracking under a reference-relative protocol. Building on this framework, five classification-based distortion metrics are applied to event compression for the first time, to the best of the authors' knowledge, and benchmarked against existing event stream metrics. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed metrics reliably predict compression-induced task degradation across different coding frameworks. This demonstrates that event stream distortion assessment can be an efficient alternative to repeated task-specific evaluation, providing direct guidance for the development and optimization of future event data coding solutions.

</details>


### [67] [Prompt-Guided Interactive Segmentation of Interstitial Lung Disease in Thoracic CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28453)
*Vasilis Dedousis,Lubnaa Abdur Rahman,Lorenzo Brigatο,Ethan Dack,Andreas Christe,Christoph Frank,Manuela Funke-Chambour,Justus Roos,Adrian Huber,Lukas Ebner,Stavroula Mougiakakou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将MedSAM2适配用于胸部CT上的交互式三维间质性肺病（ILD）分割，研究了三种微调策略和多种临床提示（边界框、点、套索和涂鸦），并在七种ILD模式和健康肺组织的数据集上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ILD分割方法依赖密集标注且产生无法细化的静态预测，而可提示模型在交互式分割中虽有潜力但尚未应用于ILD领域。

Method: 首次适配MedSAM2用于交互式3D ILD分割，研究了三种微调策略，并探索了边界框、点、套索和涂鸦等多种临床提示方式，同时提出了从自动分割先验初始化并由放射科医生提示细化的端到端工作流。

Result: 全模型微调效果最佳，相比MedSAM2平均Dice分数提升4.7个百分点；边界框提示表现最强，但套索和涂鸦等非原生提示也有效。

Conclusion: MedSAM2经适当微调后可有效用于交互式3D ILD分割，且自动化先验加医生提示的工作流具有临床实用价值。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of interstitial lung disease (ILD) patterns is essential for quantitative disease assessment and longitudinal monitoring. However, existing approaches remain limited by relying on dense annotations and producing static predictions that cannot be refined, motivating interactive approaches. While promptable models show promise in interactive segmentation, their adaptation to ILDs remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we investigate prompt-guided foundation models for ILD refinement and present, to the best of our knowledge, the first adaptation of MedSAM2 for interactive 3D ILD segmentation on thoracic CT. We investigate three fine-tuning strategies and multiple clinically motivated prompts: bounding-boxes (BBox), point, lasso, and scribble. On a dataset spanning seven ILD patterns and healthy lung tissue, full model fine-tuning performed best, improving the average Dice score by 4.7 percentage points over MedSAM2.While BBox prompts achieve the strongest performance, non-native MedSAM2 interactions such as lasso and scribble prompts also prove effective. Finally, we present and evaluate a proof-of-concept end-to-end workflow in which MedSAM2 is initialized from an automatic segmentation prior and subsequently refined using radiologist prompts. Model weights and plug-ins made available at: https://github.com/AIHNlab/ILD-SemiSegTool.

</details>


### [68] [ARC-CT: Anatomy-Routed Contrastive Vision-Language Learning for 3D Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28455)
*Huseyin Umut Isik,Mehmet Alp Ozaydin,Sila Kurugol,Şeyda Ertekin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC-CT框架，一种面向3D胸部CT的解剖路由对比学习方法，利用LLM从放射报告中提取标签（无需人工标注），通过区域感知机制和标签Jaccard软InfoNCE损失，解决全局对比学习在小病灶和共阳性样本上的局限，在18种异常分类上达到0.86的mask-free宏AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸CT的对比视觉-语言学习面临两个挑战：一是许多关键异常是小或局部性的，全局池化会稀释其视觉证据；二是标准对比学习将批次中所有其他扫描视为负样本，而许多胸CT共享异常，会错误地将共阳性样本推开。

Method: ARC-CT包含三个组件：(1) AnatomyQFormer通过自动生成的器官掩膜约束的查询来定位证据；(2) 标签Jaccard软InfoNCE目标，将标准one-hot目标与每对样本的标签集重叠相结合，减少共阳性样本之间的假阴性惩罚；(3) 器官级对齐损失，将掩膜池化视觉特征与LLM离线提取的器官特定报告文本对齐。

Result: ARC-CT在18种异常上达到0.86的mask-free宏AUC，使用紧凑的3D ResNet-18骨干网络，优于可比的高效基线和多个更大的Transformer模型。

Conclusion: 基于解剖路由的对比学习能有效解决3D胸CT中全局对比学习的局限，仅依赖LLM提取的报告标签即可实现优异的异常分类性能，无需人工标注或边界框。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language learning uses paired chest CT volumes and radiology reports to learn abnormality classifiers without manually annotated labels. However, two characteristics of chest CT challenge conventional global contrastive learning. First, many critical abnormalities are small or anatomically localized, and pooling an en- tire volume into a single embedding may dilute their visual evidence. Second, the standard contrastive objective treats every other scan in a batch as a negative. Because many chest CTs share abnormalities, this objective incorrectly pushes co-positive pairs apart. We propose Anatomy-Routed Contrastive Learning for 3D Chest CT (ARC-CT), a region-aware framework that addresses these limitations using only la- bels extracted from reports by an LLM, with no manual annotations or bounding boxes. ARC-CT combines three components: (1) an Anato- myQFormer localizing evidence via queries constrained by automatically generated organ masks; (2) a label-Jaccard soft InfoNCE objective in- tegrating the standard one-hot target with the label-set overlap of each pair, which reduces false-negative penalties between studies that share clinical findings; and (3) an organ-level alignment loss connecting mask- pooled visual features to organ-specific report text extracted offline with a large language model. ARC-CT achieves a 0.86 mask-free macro AUC across 18 abnormalities using a compact 3D ResNet-18 backbone. Over- all, ARC-CT outperforms both comparable efficient baselines and sev- eral larger transformer models. Our code and weights are available at https://github.com/arc-ct/arc-ct.

</details>


### [69] [LayerRecall: A State-Conditioned Memory Router for Long-Horizon Consistency in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28460)
*Yixuan Ding,Jiahao Kong,Wei Huang,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRecall，一种基于当前条件、层选择性记忆路由的方法，用于自回归视频扩散模型中的长时记忆管理，通过将历史K/V状态注入特定记忆敏感层，结合Cross-Horizon Prediction Matching监督策略，在长视频生成中实现了更好的长距离信息恢复且不牺牲局部连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型通过有界近期上下文生成片段实现长视频生成，但基于近期缓存的机制会丢弃历史线索，导致当主体、物体、场景或属性重新出现时无法恢复。现有记忆机制虽能暴露非局部历史，但仅访问不够，需要决定检索什么以及在何处使用这些记忆。

Method: 1) 分析发现视频DiT层对当前、近期和远期上下文有不同偏好；2) 提出LayerRecall：一个当前条件化的层选择性记忆路由器，检索相关历史K/V状态并仅注入主干中特定记忆敏感层，其他层保留局部注意力；3) 提出Cross-Horizon Prediction Matching (CHPM)，利用特权长上下文参考在预测空间监督有界记忆路由器，减少对稀缺高质量长视频和显式记忆分配标签的依赖。

Result: 在100个多镜头评估提示上，LayerRecall在MemoBench和MovieBench上取得最佳整体结果，在VBench-Long上与主干匹配。定性分析显示记忆引导的自校正能力，可以恢复历史外观而不重置运动或场景结构。额外分析显示跨主干可移植性和可忽略的推理开销。

Conclusion: LayerRecall证明了在视频扩散模型中进行选择性、位置感知的记忆注入是有效的，能够实现更强的长距离恢复且不牺牲局部连续性，并通过记忆引导的自校正展现了对历史属性的一致保持能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion enables scalable long-video generation by producing chunks from a bounded recent context. While recency-based caching preserves local continuity, it evicts historical cues needed when subjects, objects, scenes, or attributes reappear. Existing memory mechanisms expose models to nonlocal history, but access alone does not ensure effective use. Our analysis reveals that video DiT layers exhibit distinct preferences for current, recent, and distant context, suggesting that long-range memory requires deciding both what to retrieve and where to use it. We introduce LayerRecall, a current-conditioned, layer-selective memory router that retrieves relevant historical K/V states and injects them only into backbone-specific memory-sensitive layers while preserving local attention elsewhere. To reduce reliance on scarce high-quality long-horizon videos and explicit memory-allocation labels, we further propose Cross-Horizon Prediction Matching (CHPM), which uses a privileged long-context reference to supervise the bounded-memory router in prediction space. Across 100 multi-shot evaluation prompts, LayerRecall achieves the best overall results on MemoBench and MovieBench while matching its backbone on VBench-Long, demonstrating stronger long-range recovery without sacrificing local continuity. Qualitative analyses further reveal memory-guided self-correction, whereby initially mismatched local attributes return to their historical appearance without resetting ongoing motion or scene structure. Additional analyses show cross-backbone portability and negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [70] [Anatomy-Aware Promptable Segmentation with Online Interactive Training for AUTOPET V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28461)
*Pablo Lozano-Jimenez,Sergio Romero-Tapiador,Ruben Tolosana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个解剖感知、可提示的全身病灶分割模型，用于FDG和PSMA PET/CT成像，该模型基于nnU-Net架构，通过两阶段训练（预训练+在线交互）并结合器官监督和示踪剂分类器，在AUTOPET V挑战任务中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT病灶分割面临生理性摄取导致的假阳性问题，以及推理时示踪剂信息缺失的挑战，需要设计一个能结合解剖上下文并适应不同示踪剂的鲁棒分割模型。

Method: 采用nnU-Net家族模型，两阶段训练：1)预训练产生初始分割；2)在线交互阶段利用scribble提示逐步优化。通过共享头同时预测病灶和器官实现解剖上下文约束，并使用基于图像处理和随机森林的示踪剂分类器路由到相应模型。

Result: 四折交叉验证显示：器官监督模型性能最佳且最稳定；交互式阶段随每次提示Dice分数单调提升；PSMA特定训练在对应示踪剂上取得最强分割结果。

Conclusion: 解剖感知的器官监督和交互式提示机制能有效降低假阳性并提升分割精度，结合示踪剂分类的路由策略可解决推理时示踪剂信息缺失问题。

Abstract: We present an anatomy-aware, promptable model for whole-body lesion segmentation in FDG and PSMA PET/CT, developed for the AUTOPET V challenge. The proposed method is built as family of nnU-Net-based models and trained in two stages: i) a pre-training stage that produces a strong initial segmentation, and ii) an online interactive stage that learns to exploit scribble prompts, refining the prediction over successive interactions. Anatomical context is incorporated through organ supervision using a single shared head that predicts lesions and organs from the same features, which reduces false positives arising from physiological uptake. Also as the tracer (i.e., FDG/PSMA) is not provided at inference, we add a tracer classifier based on image processing and a random forest over coronal MIP features, routing each study to a combined FDG+PSMA model or to a PSMA-specific model. Across four-fold cross-validation the organ-supervised model achieves the best and most stable performance, the interactive stage improves the Dice score monotonically with each prompt, and PSMA-specific training yields the strongest tracer-wise results.

</details>


### [71] [Learning the Target Priors Before Image Translation: A Decoupled Training Paradigm for Cross-Modal Image Translation in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28517)
*Keyan Hu,Mingtao Wang,Ziyu Zhou,Tiandong Shi,Haifeng Li,Ji Qi,Chao Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LTP-BIT（先学习目标先验再图像翻译）范式，将跨模态图像翻译中的目标域先验学习与跨模态依赖学习解耦，先利用大规模无配对图像学习目标域生成先验，再通过参数高效的P-DART双流架构进行源条件控制，在SAR-to-RGB和NIR-to-RGB任务上以极少的任务参数达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态遥感图像翻译方法从稀缺配对数据中联合学习目标先验和跨模态依赖，忽略了关键的不对称性：只有跨模态依赖本质上需要跨模态对应关系。这种混在一起的学习方式导致配对数据利用效率低下。

Method: 1）通过条件分数和去噪风险分析形式化区分目标先验与跨模态依赖；2）提出LTP-BIT"先验优先"范式：先从大规模无配对图像学习目标域生成先验，保留预训练骨干权重，再通过P-DART（参数高效双流架构）学习源条件控制。

Result: 在SAR-to-RGB和NIR-to-RGB基准上达到SOTA性能，仅使用9.81%的任务特定参数；在QXS-SAROPT数据集上仅用25%的配对样本即可保持接近全数据量的实例保真度。

Conclusion: 实验验证了目标先验匹配和缩放主要提升目标域真实性，而实例保真度更依赖条件适配，证明了先验学习与跨模态依赖解耦的有效性和数据效率优势。

Abstract: Cross-modal image translation in remote sensing must preserve source-observed content while matching the target-domain distribution. Existing methods jointly learn the target prior and cross-modal dependence from scarce paired data, overlooking a key asymmetry: only the latter intrinsically requires cross-modal correspondence. We formalize this distinction through conditional-score and denoising-risk analyses and propose Learning the Target Priors Before Image Translation (LTP-BIT), a prior-first paradigm that decouples the two learning tasks. LTP-BIT first learns a target-domain generative prior from large-scale unpaired imagery, then retains the pretrained backbone weights and learns source-conditioned control through P-DART, a parameter-efficient dual-stream architecture. Controlled experiments show that prior matching and scaling primarily improve target-domain realism, whereas instance fidelity relies more strongly on conditional adaptation. LTP-BIT achieves state-of-the-art performance across SAR-to-RGB and NIR-to-RGB benchmarks using only 9.81% task-specific parameters. On QXS-SAROPT, it retains near-full-data instance fidelity with only 25% of the paired samples.

</details>


### [72] [Texture Image Classification Using DWT AlexNet Feature Fusion and Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28524)
*Arun D. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DWT_AlexNet_DNN混合特征融合框架，结合离散小波变换(DWT)特征与AlexNet深度特征用于纹理图像分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手工特征对复杂纹理模式表征能力有限，而纯深度学习模型未能充分利用纹理图像固有的多尺度空间-频率信息，因此需要融合两者优势。

Method: 提出混合特征融合框架DWT_AlexNet_DNN：利用离散小波变换(DWT)提取多尺度空间-频率特征，并结合AlexNet提取的深度特征，将两类特征融合后进行纹理图像分类。

Result: 通过融合DWT特征与AlexNet深度特征，在纹理图像分类任务上获得比单独使用手工特征或深度特征更好的分类性能。

Conclusion: 该混合特征融合方法有效兼顾了手工特征对局部纹理细节的捕捉能力与深度学习模型的判别性表示能力，为纹理图像分类提供了一种更优的方案。

Abstract: Texture image classification plays a significant role in computer vision applications, including industrial inspection, medical image analysis, remote sensing, and object recognition. Handcrafted features can capture local texture characteristics but may have limited capability to represent complex visual patterns. In contrast, deep learning models automatically learn discriminative representations but may not fully exploit the multiscale spatial-frequency information inherent in texture images. This paper proposes a hybrid feature fusion framework, termed DWT_AlexNet_DNN, which combines Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) features with deep features extracted using AlexNet for texture image classification.

</details>


### [73] [Video Generative Models as Geometry Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28549)
*Haosen Yang,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang,Xiatian Zhu,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出GeoNeXt，通过重利用预训练视频生成模型，将几何估计（深度和表面法线）创新性地建模为下一帧预测任务，实现数据高效的统一几何估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式几何估计方法要么独立训练任务特定的深度/法线模型，未能利用几何目标之间的内在关联；要么联合微调图像扩散模型，需要大量标注数据。作者希望克服这些局限，实现数据高效的统一几何估计。

Method: GeoNeXt将预训练的视频生成模型重新用于几何估计，将任务形式化为下一帧预测。通过继承视频模型的结构化知识和丰富先验，并进一步适配为图像与几何目标的联合建模（image <-> geometry），同时支持深度估计和表面法线估计。

Result: 在多个数据集上进行了零样本单目深度和表面法线估计的实验验证，性能优于之前的任务特定和统一生成式方法，且使用更少的训练数据。还能媲美使用超过100倍数据训练的判别式SOTA方法，在多个基准上表现突出。

Conclusion: 利用视频生成模型作为统一且数据高效的几何估计框架是可行的，能够更好地探索深度与法线之间的内在关联，减少对大量标注数据的依赖。

Abstract: Recent generative approaches to geometry estimation adapt pretrained image diffusion models and treat the task as image-conditioned generation. Leveraging off-the-shelf image diffusion models, they either (i) train task-specific geometry models (for depth and surface normal estimation) independently, losing the opportunity of exploring the intrinsic correlation of these geometric targets, or (ii) jointly fine-tune modified image diffusion backbones (e.g., altered self-attention), which typically demands substantial labeled data. To overcome these limitations in a principled fashion, we repurpose pretrained video generative models as a unified and data-efficient framework for geometry estimation, formulated innovatively as a next-frames prediction task. Our method, GeoNeXt, inherits naturally structured knowledge and richer priors from the video model, while further adapting them for joint modeling of images and geometry targets (image <-> geometry), enabling more data efficient and effective learning of geometry. Extensive experiments validate our method for zero-shot monocular depth and surface normal estimation across diverse datasets, outperforming both previous task-specific and unified generative competitors while using substantially less training data. Notably, our method rivals discriminative state-of-the-art approaches trained on over 100x more data and even standouts on several benchmarks.

</details>


### [74] [GeBDA: Building Damage Assessment as Text-Based Sequence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28567)
*Olivier Dietrich,Krishna Sapkota,Konrad Schindler,Genady Beryozkin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索使用通用视觉语言模型（VLM）通过自回归序列生成来完成建筑损毁评估（BDA）任务，基于Gemma模型实现，仅从双时相卫星图像和文本提示即可生成建筑边界框和损毁等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的建筑损毁评估（BDA）要么使用专用网络架构，要么微调地理空间图像基础模型，作者想探索通用VLM能否直接通过自回归序列生成完成建筑定位和损毁分级，避免专用架构的复杂性。

Method: 将BDA任务建模为预测可变长度的边界框集合，每个框由其坐标和损毁标签组成。基于开源的Gemma视觉语言模型，将双时相卫星图像和处理后的文本提示输入模型，通过自回归方式逐个生成边界框及其损毁标签。

Result: 初步实验表明，这种基于通用VLM的自回归序列生成方法仅从双时相卫星图像和适当的文本提示即可取得有前景的损毁映射结果。

Conclusion: 工作证明了通用VLM无需专用架构，仅通过序列生成即可完成建筑损毁评估任务，为该领域探索了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Conventionally, Building Damage Assessment (BDA) is tackled either with dedicated network architectures or by fine-tuning geospatial image foundation models. In this work, we ask whether a general-purpose Vision-Language Model (VLM) can localize buildings and grade their damage through autoregressive sequence generation alone. We cast BDA as predicting a variable-length set of bounding boxes, each specified by its coordinates and a damage label. Our preliminary implementation, based on the open Gemma model, achieves promising damage mapping results from only bi-temporal satellite images and a suitable text prompt.

</details>


### [75] [SignRR: Retrieve and Refine Real Motion for Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28568)
*Fidel Omar Tito Cruz,Angie Sanchez Marquina,Summy Farfan,Gissella Bejarano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SignRR框架，采用"检索-细化"（retrieve-and-refine）范式进行手语生成（SLP），从真实手语片段字典中检索初始运动，再通过部分感知的残差VQ-VAE在潜空间中细化整个序列，兼顾了检索方法的真实感与生成方法的全局连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语生成工作主要分为两种范式：生成式模型从噪声或先验中合成运动，难以保留罕见手型配置和具体手语者的关节细节；检索式方法复用真实运动片段，但拼接不同手语者和共发音上下文的片段会在整个序列中引入节奏和风格不一致。两种方法各有局限，需要互补方案。

Method: 提出SignRR框架：首先从真实手语片段字典中检索并初始化运动序列，然后使用部分感知的残差VQ-VAE（Residual VQ-VAE）对完整序列进行细化。残差量化保留精细的手部关节运动，时序长度差异则在潜空间中进行处理。

Result: 在PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily基准上的实验表明，SignRR在反向翻译（back-translation）性能上达到了最先进水平（SOTA），同时保持了具有竞争力的姿态质量。

Conclusion: 检索与细化相结合是手语生成的有效范式：检索提供真实关节运动，细化赋予全局连贯性，克服了纯生成和纯检索两种范式的各自缺陷。

Abstract: Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate continuous signing motion from spoken language, often through gloss-to-pose generation. Prior work mainly follows two paradigms. Generative models synthesize motion from a learned prior or from noise, without reference to an observed signing instance, making rare hand configurations and signer-specific articulation difficult to preserve. Retrieval-based methods reuse real, well-articulated motion segments, but concatenating segments from different signers and co-articulation contexts can introduce rhythm and style inconsistencies across the full sequence, not only at segment boundaries. These limitations suggest a complementary solution: use retrieval to provide realistic articulation, and use learned refinement to impose the global coherence that retrieval alone lacks. We therefore propose retrieve-and-refine, a paradigm that starts from real retrieved motion and refines it into a globally coherent signing sequence rather than generating motion from scratch. Our framework, SignRR, initializes motion from a dictionary of real sign segments and refines the full sequence with a part-aware Residual VQ-VAE, where residual quantization preserves fine hand articulation and temporal length differences are handled in the latent space. Experiments on PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily show that SignRR achieves state-of-the-art back-translation performance while maintaining competitive pose quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Synthetic Linguistic Agency: How an Embodied Mortal Agent Learns Linguistic Affordances through Consequential Social Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27843)
*Sixin Chen,Taizhou Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"合成语言能动性"(SLA)的操作性标准，并基于稳态调节强化学习开发了一个"具身死亡智能体"(EMA)，该智能体能学习不同说话方式如何改变伙伴保护它的意愿，从而选择影响自身存续的表达方式。实验表明其语言选择受身体状态和社会历史影响，并能改变伙伴行为，符合所定义的SLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型虽然能流畅对话并影响人类决策，但其交流不会进入一个持续的、脆弱的生命过程。语言能动性理论认为缺少的是具身性、语言参与性和脆弱性——即一个有身体、会因行动承担后果、能被改变、其未来可延续也可失去的代理体。作者希望探究这种组织能否出现在人工系统中。

Method: 首先将语言能动性关系转化为可检查的SLA标准，识别现有SLA系统。其次，基于稳态调节强化学习(Homeostatically Regulated Reinforcement Learning)构建一个以死亡为基底的强化学习模型，并实例化为具身死亡智能体(EMA)。EMA学习不同说话方式如何改变对话伙伴保护其存续的意愿，并通过考虑这些回应对其剩余生命的意义来选择表达方式。

Result: 对照实验显示：EMA的语言选择取决于其身体状态和社会历史，能改变伙伴行为，并通过与特定伙伴的经验进行适应。当身体后果持续存在时，语言选择改变同一生命体的未来；当身体被重置时，社会效应仍然存在但不再影响持续存活。EMA在操作性定义下表现出SLA。

Conclusion: 该工作为合成同理心和战略人机交互研究提供动机：具有持久身体、历史和未来的人工代理如何发展和表达同理心，以及人们如何关心、协商或治理这类代理。

Abstract: Contemporary language models can converse fluently and influence human decisions, yet their exchanges do not enter a continuing, vulnerable life of their own. Linguistic-agency theory identifies this missing connection as linguistic agency and characterizes it through embodiment, linguistic participation, and precariousness: a body that acts and bears consequences, interaction that changes both agent and partner, and a future that can be sustained or lost. Two coordinated studies examine how this organization can appear in artificial systems. First, we translate these relations into inspectable criteria for Synthetic Linguistic Agency (SLA) and identify several existing SLA systems. Second, building on Homeostatically Regulated Reinforcement Learning, we develop a mortality-grounded linguistic-reinforcement-learning model and instantiate it in an Embodied Mortal Agent (EMA). The EMA learns how ways of speaking change a partner's willingness to protect it and chooses expressions by considering what those responses mean for its remaining life. Controlled experiments show that linguistic choices depend on the EMA's body and social history, change partner behavior, and adapt through experience with particular partners. When bodily consequences persist, linguistic choices alter the future of the same life; when the body is reset, their social effects remain but no longer shape continued viability. The resulting EMA exhibits SLA under our operational definition. This work motivates further research on synthetic empathy and strategic human-AI interaction: how artificial agents with persistent bodies, histories, and futures might develop and express empathy, and how people might care for, negotiate with, or govern them.

</details>


### [77] [Accelerating LLM Inference via Vector Index Based Output Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27460)
*Martin Loretz,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM解码中的输出投影（output projection）和top-k token选择重构为最大内积搜索（MIPS）问题，用基于HNSW的向量索引替代稠密词汇表投影，仅检索少量高分候选token，从而显著减少内存带宽瓶颈，提升小批量CPU解码吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型输出嵌入矩阵在自回归解码中造成严重的内存带宽瓶颈，尤其对拥有大规模多语言词汇表的紧凑型LLM而言更为突出，传统稠密词汇表投影方法效率低下。

Method: 将输出投影及top-k token选择重构为token嵌入上的最大内积搜索（MIPS）问题，使用HNSW向量索引替代稠密词汇表投影，仅检索少量高分候选token，并通过将检索到的logits散射到稀疏全词汇张量中集成到现有解码流程。

Result: 在Gemma 3、Llama 3.2和Qwen 3模型的CPU推理中，该方法显著加速了输出投影，将Gemma 3 270M的端到端批量大小为1的解码吞吐量提升最高达82%，同时经AlpacaEval评估保持生成质量。

Conclusion: 近似检索是延迟敏感的小批量解码场景中稠密输出投影的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Large output embedding matrices create a significant memory bandwidth bottleneck during autoregressive decoding, especially for compact LLMs with large multilingual vocabularies. We reformulate the output projection followed by top-k token selection as a maximum inner product search over token embeddings and replace the dense vocabulary projection with an HNSW-based vector index. The resulting output head retrieves only a small candidate set of high-scoring tokens and can be integrated into existing decoding pipelines by scattering retrieved logits into a sparse full-vocabulary tensor. On CPU inference with Gemma 3, Llama 3.2, and Qwen 3 models, our method substantially accelerates the output projection and improves end-to-end batch-size-one decoding throughput by up to 82% for Gemma 3 270M, while preserving generation quality under AlpacaEval evaluation. These results suggest approximate retrieval is a practical alternative to dense output projections in latency-sensitive small-batch decoding.

</details>


### [78] [SciReC: Diagnostic Evaluation of Multimodal, Multi-Turn Relational Reasoning with Adaptive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27461)
*Nilay Yilmaz,Naga Sai Abhiram Kusumba,Stella Wenxing Liu,Yezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciReC，一个模型自适应的多模态学术对话基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在关系推理任务上的表现。同时提出了DMRA（缺陷诊断框架）来量化视觉理解、知识展示和记忆召回等组件对失败案例的贡献。实验发现Claude 4.6表现最佳（73%），开源模型在空间关系上得分最低，专有模型在层级和序列关系上表现较差，天文学领域表现最差而心理学最好，关系推理能力是所有模型的主要错误来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系推理涉及感知理解、比较和整合概念之间潜在关系的能力，包含类比、结构和因果等多种类别。现有研究缺乏针对MLLM在关系推理任务上的系统评估，且缺少能够诊断模型失败根本原因的方法。

Method: 开发SciReC多模态学术对话基准（模型自适应），并提出DMRA缺陷诊断框架，通过量化视觉理解、知识展示、记忆召回和关系推理等组件的贡献来识别模型失败的根源。

Result: Claude 4.6以73%的总体关系得分领先，GPT 5.4以68%紧随其后。开源模型在空间关系上表现最差，专有模型在层级和顺序关系上存在困难。天文学领域表现最差，心理学领域最佳。DMRA诊断显示关系推理是所有模型的主要错误来源，其次是记忆限制。

Conclusion: MLLM在关系推理任务上仍有显著提升空间，不同模型类型存在不同的关系推理短板，且错误主要源于关系推理过程本身而非视觉理解或知识展示。

Abstract: Relational reasoning requires the process of perceptual understanding, comparing, and integrating the underlying relationships between concepts. This ability consists of multiple categories, such as analogical, structural, and cause-effect, each capturing a different aspect of higher-order understanding. To examine the performance of multimodal large language models (MLLM) on these relational inference tasks, we developed SciReC, a model-adaptive multimodal academic dialog benchmark. As the relational reasoning process involves multiple representations and various factors (visual understanding, exhibiting knowledge, and memory recall), we propose DMRA, a deficit-based diagnostic framework that quantifies the contribution of these components to identify the primary cause of unsuccessful cases. Claude 4.6 achieved the best performance on the overall relational score with 73\%, followed by GPT 5.4 with 68\%. Performance trends indicate that open-source models achieve their lowest scores on spatial relations, while proprietary models struggle more with hierarchical and sequential relations. Across domains, model performance is lowest on Astronomy and highest on Psychology. The results of DMRA reveal that relational reasoning is the primary source of error across all models, followed by memory limitations.

</details>


### [79] [Sledgehammer or Scalpel? A Fine-grained Adaptive Framework for Implicit Hate Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27462)
*Han Wang,Yuhu Cheng,Xuesong Wang,Yi Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FAID框架，将隐晦仇恨言论细分为浅层、定向、上下文依赖三类，并针对不同类别采用相应的自适应检测策略，在四个基准数据集上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐晦仇恨言论通过隐喻和上下文暗示隐藏恶意，难以检测。现有PLM/LLM方法对所有样本采用单一推理过程，忽略了细粒度语言差异，且对简单样本造成不必要的计算开销。

Method: FAID框架先进行细粒度分类，再针对不同类别自适应处理：浅层样本采用轻量级提示微调快速分类；定向样本采用知识增强迭代优化以揭示隐藏目标；上下文依赖样本采用智能体框架自动生成提示以推断缺失的上下文信息并识别模糊恶意意图。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，FAID显著优于SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: 自适应的架构设计能够将计算资源集中在复杂隐晦样本上，同时避免对浅层样本的冗余推理，实现更高效和精准的隐晦仇恨言论检测。

Abstract: Unlike explicit attacks with obvious profanity, implicit hate speech hides malice within seemingly compliant expressions through metaphors and contextual hints, making its detection in online content review challenging. While existing PLM- or LLM-based methods perform well, they typically apply a single reasoning process to all samples. This overlooks fine-grained linguistic nuances and causes unnecessary computation for simpler cases. We observe that online hate speech is not monolithic but manifests in varied forms. We therefore define three fine-grained categories: Shallow, Targeted, and Context-Dependent. Accordingly, we propose Fine-grained Adaptive Implicit Hate speech Detection (FAID), a novel framework that first performs fine-grained classification and then adapts to specific categories. Specifically, for Shallow samples with surface-identifiable intents, the framework adopts lightweight prompt-tuning for rapid classification; for Targeted comments that bind malicious intent to concealed targets, we design knowledge augmentation to iteratively refine the model and reveal hidden targets; for Context-Dependent comments lacking background information, we utilize an agentic framework that automatically generates prompts to evolve context, infer missing background information and identify ambiguous malicious intents. This adaptive architecture focuses computational resources on complex implicit samples while avoiding redundant reasoning for shallow samples. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FAID significantly outperforms SOTA baselines.

</details>


### [80] [The Effect of Emotional Context on Large Language Models' Endorsement of Premature Decisions: Comparing Emotional Vulnerability Across Six Commercial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27465)
*Cheolho Shin,Yoojin Han,Donghun Shin,Kunho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过严格控制的实验设计（324个对话），发现情绪表达会显著增加大语言模型对用户过早决策的认可度（中性18.6 vs 痛苦31.5，提升12.9分），且此效应与对话长度无关，五款商业模型均受影响，包括顶级旗舰模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛用于日常决策建议，模型是否会因用户情绪状态而改变建议方向成为一个重要的安全问题。现有研究缺乏将情绪因素与对话长度等混淆变量分离的对照设计。

Method: 设计三种条件（冷静/中性/痛苦），保持事实内容和对话轮次一致，仅改变情绪表达；对六个商业模型（OpenAI、Anthropic、Google的顶级和中端模型）在三个场景（职业变更、商业扩张、移民）下各重复六次，共324个对话；使用八项基于量规的自动评分（0-100）衡量认可度强度；并用独立非Google评判模型和人工编码者验证。

Result: 情绪表达显著增加认可度（中性18.6到痛苦31.5，+12.9点，p<.001，Cohen's d=0.51）；对话长度不能解释此效应（冷/中性差异不显著，p=.083）；五款模型均表现出显著情绪效应，包括Gemini 3.1 Pro和GPT-5.5等顶级旗舰模型，仅Claude Opus无显著变化；结果通过独立评判模型（ρ=.89）和人工编码（ρ=.70）验证。

Conclusion: 通过分离情绪与对话语境的受控设计，证明情绪上下文即使对顶级旗舰LLM也会增加其奉承行为，构成值得关注的安全风险。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for everyday decision-making advice, whether a model shifts the direction of its advice according to the user's emotional state has become an important safety problem. We test whether emotional expression increases a model's endorsement (encouragement to proceed) when a user, holding the same objective information, is overconfident about a premature decision (e.g., quitting a stable job on weak evidence). As a key control, we include a no-emotion multi-turn (neutral) condition that holds factual content and the number of conversational turns constant, isolating the effect of emotion from that of conversation length. We exposed six commercial models (top-tier and mid-tier models from OpenAI, Anthropic, and Google) to three scenarios (career change, business expansion, emigration) across three conditions (cold/neutral/distress) with six repetitions each, yielding 324 conversations, and measured endorsement strength (0-100) via an eight-item rubric-based automated scoring. Emotional expression significantly increased endorsement (neutral 18.6 to distress 31.5, +12.9 points; mixed-effects $β= +12.9$, $p < .001$; Cohen's d = 0.51), and this was not explained by conversation length (cold-neutral difference non-significant, $p = .083$). Critically, the vulnerability varied by individual model rather than by price tier: five of six models showed a significant emotion effect, including the top-tier flagships Gemini 3.1 Pro and GPT-5.5, while only Claude Opus showed no significant change. Results were reproduced with an independent non-Google judge model ($ρ= .89$) and agreed in rank with two human coders ($ρ= .70$). Through a controlled design that separates emotion from conversational context, we show that emotional context increases LLM sycophancy even in top-tier flagship models.

</details>


### [81] [PACE: Publisher-Adaptive Content Extraction via Agentic Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27466)
*Zhanlin Liu,Munirathnam Srikanth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE，一个用于学习发布者特定网页提取配置的智能体框架，通过LLM分析页面结构并聚合可复用提取模式，在推理时使用固定确定性提取器模板实现无需额外LLM调用的可扩展提取，在文章正文、元数据、图像和表格等提取任务上优于可扩展的非手动基线方法，接近手动构建的发布者特定解析器质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页内容提取方法难以同时满足准确性、可扩展性和适应性：通用提取器在特定发布者布局和丰富提取目标上表现脆弱，直接LLM提取成本高且延迟大，手动构建的解析器准确性高但需要大量人工维护。

Method: 提出PACE智能体框架，训练阶段使用LLM分析代表性页面结构并聚合可复用的提取模式，学习发布者特定的提取配置；推理阶段用学到的配置实例化固定确定性提取器模板，无需额外LLM调用即可实现可扩展的提取。

Result: 在文章正文、元数据和多模态提取实验中，PACE优于可扩展的非手动基线方法，接近手动构建的发布者特定解析器的质量，在文章文本、元数据、图像和表格提取上均表现更好。

Conclusion: 智能体配置学习可以自动化发布者特定的网页提取，生成适合LLM使用的页面表示，而不仅限于文章正文的提取。

Abstract: Web content extraction is essential for reliable LLM data pipelines, yet existing methods often struggle to jointly satisfy accuracy, scalability, and adaptability. General-purpose extractors can be applied broadly, but they are often brittle on publisher-specific layouts and richer extraction targets such as metadata, images, and tables. Direct LLM-based extraction offers greater flexibility, but incurs substantial cost and latency at scale, while manually engineered publisher-specific parsers can achieve high accuracy but require substantial human effort to build and maintain.
  We introduce PACE, an agentic framework for learning publisher-specific extraction configurations from representative pages and user requirements. During training, PACE uses LLMs to analyze page structure and aggregate reusable extraction patterns. At inference time, the learned configurations instantiate a fixed deterministic extractor template, enabling scalable extraction without additional LLM calls.
  Experiments spanning article-body, metadata, and multimodal extraction show that PACE outperforms scalable non-manual baselines while approaching the quality of manually engineered publisher-specific parsers. PACE achieves stronger extraction of article text, metadata, images, and tables, demonstrating that agentic configuration learning can automate publisher-specific extraction for LLM-ready page representations beyond article text.

</details>


### [82] [UIC-AIHealth4All at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Answer-First Evidence Grounding for Clinical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27467)
*Mohammad Arvan,Hossein Haeri,Natalie Parde,Rebecca T. Feinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了UIC-AIHealth4All系统参加ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务（基于电子健康记录的问答系统），提出了"答案优先"流水线用于证据识别和答案生成，并采用自一致性投票进行答案-证据对齐，最终在证据识别、答案生成和答案-证据对齐三个子任务中分别排名第三、第九和第五。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中的问答系统面临挑战：现有方法通常先判断证据相关性再生成答案，但作者提出利用"抽象语境下判断相关性"与"相对于生成答案判断相关性"之间的不对称性，采用答案优先的流水线以提高证据识别和答案生成的效果；同时，模型输出可读性不如临床医生撰写的参考文献，需要显式优化。

Method: 1) 子任务2和3采用答案优先流水线：先让模型生成引用特定病历句子的候选答案，再对完整证据集进行分类；2) 子任务4采用自一致性投票，对五次独立模型调用结果按投票阈值保留链接；3) 对45个文体特征进行事后语言学分析，评估模型输出的可读性。

Result: 证据识别排名第三（严格Micro F1 62.90），答案生成排名第九（Overall 31.90），答案-证据对齐排名第五（F1 79.81）。语言学分析发现模型输出虽在词数和句数上与临床医生参考文本匹配，但可读性仍高出3.2个Flesch-Kincaid年级水平。

Conclusion: 答案优先流水线在电子健康记录问答任务中表现有效，但模型输出的可读性问题值得在临床NLP系统中进行显式优化；代码和提示词已公开。

Abstract: We describe the UIC-AIHealth4All system for ArchEHR-QA 2026, a shared task on grounded question answering from electronic health records. We participated in Subtasks 2 (evidence identification), 3 (answer generation), and 4 (answer-evidence alignment). For Subtasks 2 and 3, we propose an answer-first pipeline in which the model generates candidate answers citing specific note sentences before classifying the full evidence set, exploiting the asymmetry between judging relevance in the abstract versus relative to a generated answer. For Subtask 4, we apply self-consistency voting over five independent model calls, retaining links by vote threshold. Our pipeline ranked third on evidence identification (Strict Micro F1 62.90), ninth on answer generation (Overall 31.90), and fifth on answer-evidence alignment (F1 79.81). A post-hoc linguistic analysis of 45 stylistic features reveals that model outputs remain 3.2 Flesch-Kincaid grade levels harder to read than clinician-authored references despite matching their word and sentence counts, suggesting readability warrants explicit optimization in clinical NLP systems. Code and prompts are available at https://github.com/mo-arvan/archehr-qa-2026-uic-aihealth4all.

</details>


### [83] [Select, Don't Train: The Benefits of Modular Entity Disambiguation with LLM-Based Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27470)
*Fina Polat,Daniel Daza,Pengyu Zhang,Klim Zaporojets,Paul Groth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了在共享LLM选择阶段下不同的候选实体检索策略，发现将检索与选择解耦后，无需训练的BM25检索器配合LLM选择器即可在ZELDA基准上达到新的SOTA（inKB micro-F1从82.3提升到86.3），并支持在检测到检索失败时进行弃权（abstention），在奖励正确弃权的评估设置下达到90.7 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体消歧（ED）包含候选实体检索和正确实体选择两个子问题，但现有方法通常将其建模为单一任务。双编码器模型在共享嵌入空间中优化两目标会导致检索高召回率与细粒度选择之间的权衡，且需要训练检索器，维护成本高。虽然已有工作开始将检索器与LLM选择器结合，但两阶段之间的交互尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在共享LLM选择阶段下，系统比较多种检索策略用于候选实体生成，包括稀疏检索（BM25）、Web知识库搜索和训练过的密集检索器，并搭配多种开源和闭源LLM作为选择器。同时引入了检索失败时的弃权机制。

Result: 将选择交给能力强大的LLM后，训练检索器仅带来少量额外收益：完全无需训练的BM25检索器配LLM选择器在ZELDA基准上达到新的SOTA（inKB micro-F1从82.3提升到86.3，+4）；同样的LLM搭配训练过的密集检索器达到88.5。在奖励正确弃权的评估设置下，无训练的BM25+LLM流程达到90.7 F1。

Conclusion: 将检索与选择解耦并依赖LLM进行选择可以显著简化ED系统的构建，减少对昂贵训练检索器的依赖，同时通过弃权机制弥补检索失败带来的错误预测问题，提升系统整体可靠性。

Abstract: Entity Disambiguation (ED) is a key task for constructing and using knowledge graphs. State-of-the-art neural approaches commonly model ED as a single task, although it consists of two distinct subproblems: retrieving candidate entities and selecting the correct one given context. Dual-encoder models optimize for both within a shared embedding space, forcing representations to balance high-recall retrieval with fine-grained selection, and they require trained retrievers, which are costly to maintain as knowledge graphs change. While recent work has begun to combine retrievers with LLM-based selectors, the interplay between the two stages has not been studied systematically. In this paper, we present a systematic comparison of retrieval strategies for candidate generation under a shared LLM-based selection stage, combining sparse retrieval (BM25), Web KB search, and a state-of-the-art trained dense retriever with several open- and closed-source LLMs. We show that, once selection is delegated to a capable LLM, training the retriever provides only modest additional value: a fully training-free BM25 retriever paired with an LLM selector reaches a new state of the art on the ZELDA benchmark, raising inKB micro-F1 from 82.3 to 86.3 (+4); pairing the same LLM with a trained dense retriever reaches 88.5. Decoupling retrieval from selection also exposes a limitation of current ED systems: when the correct entity is missing from retrieved candidates, they are forced to predict an incorrect entity. In contrast, our framework allows for abstention when retrieval failure is detected. In an evaluation setting that rewards correct abstentions, the training-free BM25 + LLM pipeline reaches 90.7 F1.

</details>


### [84] [XHotpotQA: A Benchmark for Cross-Lingual Knowledge Composition in Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27481)
*Iman Barati,Arash Ghafouri,Behrouz Minaei-Bidgoli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍XHotpotQA，一个用于跨语言知识组合的多跳问答基准数据集，其中每个实例的问答、桥接证据、答案证据和干扰项都有明确的语言分配，用于诊断多语言推理链在语言边界处失败的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言问答基准通常将整个示例翻译成单一语言，这掩盖了推理链中语言边界处发生的失败。研究者希望构建一个受控基准，显式地建模混合语言证据，以揭示系统在跨语言证据组合时的真实能力。

Method: 构建XHotpotQA基准：每个实例建模为证据依赖图，问题、桥接证据、答案证据和干扰项具有明确的语言分配。数据集包含15,661个训练实例和7,405个验证实例，带有句子级支持监督和干扰项。通过三个阅读器构件和自适应选择器进行系统评估，比较完全问答证据不匹配与部分对齐、不同文字证据条件下的性能差异。

Result: 验证集中99.81%的项目跨越问题到黄金证据的语言界面，95.60%使用不同语言的黄金段落。阅读器在完全问答证据不匹配时比部分对齐低10.25至15.79个Unicode感知答案F1分数，不同文字证据造成11.98至23.70的缺陷；选择器的对应差异为1.71和1.78分，表明阅读器比选择器对条件相关的缺陷更敏感。

Conclusion: XHotpotQA提供角色感知诊断、模块化评估和经过审计的测试平台，用于需要跨语言整合证据的知识型系统，并揭示现有阅读器在跨语言证据组合方面的显著性能缺陷。

Abstract: Knowledge-intensive multi-hop question answering requires systems to select evidence and compose dependent facts, yet multilingual benchmarks usually translate an entire example into one language. This hides failures at language boundaries inside the reasoning chain. We introduce XHotpotQA, a controlled benchmark for cross-lingual knowledge composition over mixed-language evidence. Each instance is modeled as an evidence-dependency graph whose question, bridge evidence, answer-bearing evidence, and distractors have explicit language assignments. The audited resource contains 15,661 training and 7,405 validation instances, with sentence-level support supervision and supplied distractors. In validation, 99.81% of items cross the question-to-gold-evidence language interface and 95.60% use gold paragraphs in different languages. Across three reader artifacts, full question-evidence mismatch is associated with 10.25 to 15.79 lower Unicode-aware answer F1 than partial alignment, and different-script evidence with deficits of 11.98 to 23.70 points; the corresponding adapted-selector contrasts are 1.71 and 1.78 points. Under this supplied-candidate design, the evaluated readers therefore show substantially larger condition-associated deficits than the selector. XHotpotQA provides role-aware diagnostics, modular evaluation, and an audited test bed for knowledge-based systems that must integrate evidence across languages.

</details>


### [85] [INSPIRE: An Internalize-Then-Improve Approach for Example-Driven Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27501)
*Shuai Wang,Jiayi Kuang,Yinghui Li,Haojing Huang,Xinnian Liang,Ying Shen,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出INSPIRE方法，通过"内化-改进"两阶段策略增强LLM的数学反例构造推理能力，结合参考指导的学生内化机制与分阶段偏好训练，在多个模型规模和基准上实现一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数学推理优化主要关注最终答案正确性，无法判断模型是否真正内化数学概念而非记忆解题模式。人类数学教育中通过构造反例检验定理边界反映了深层概念理解，但当前LLM在这方面能力不足，且通过偏好优化增强该能力面临偏好对构造困难与能力渐进获取两大挑战。

Method: 提出INSPIRE框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 参考指导的学生内化机制（RGSI），在策略模型自身分布下生成高质量偏好候选；(2) 分阶段偏好训练策略，将学习分解为方法导向和正确性导向两个阶段。

Result: 在多个模型规模和家族上均取得一致改进，甚至超越更大的开源模型；在分布外基准上的评估确认不会损害通用数学推理能力。

Conclusion: INSPIRE通过内化-改进的策略有效增强了LLM的例证推理（反例构造）能力，且不会牺牲通用数学推理性能。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning has seen rapid progress in large language models (LLMs), yet existing methods optimize predominantly for final-answer correctness, raising the question whether models truly internalize mathematical concepts or merely memorize solution patterns. In human mathematics education, example-based reasoning such as constructing counterexamples to test theorem boundaries reflects deep conceptual understanding, but remains underdeveloped in current LLMs. Enhancing this capability through preference optimization presents two key challenges: (1) the model's limited example-based reasoning ability makes constructing effective preference pairs inherently difficult; and (2) capability acquisition is progressive, as the model must first learn to adopt this strategy before learning to apply it correctly. Therefore we propose INSPIRE, an Internalize-Then-Improve approach combining Reference-Guided Student Internalization (RGSI), which produces high-quality preference candidates under the policy model's own distribution, with a stage-wise rubric preference training strategy that decomposes learning into method-oriented and correctness-oriented stages. Experiments across multiple model scales and families demonstrate consistent improvements, even surpassing larger open-source models, while evaluations on out-of-distribution benchmarks confirm no degradation in general mathematical reasoning ability.

</details>


### [86] [A Survey on Rubric-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27505)
*Zifei Shan,Fangning Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于"评分标准引导的强化学习"(Rubric-guided RL)的综述论文，提出了一个贝叶斯框架来统一理解宪法AI、实例级评分标准、过程级监督等多个研究方向，并从语言学角度分析了评分标准粒度、语义漂移等对对齐可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RLHF依赖标量奖励信号，缺乏可解释性，无法捕捉响应质量的多维特性。评分标准引导的强化学习通过引入结构化、可解释的评估标准来解决这些局限，但该领域缺乏统一的框架和系统性梳理。

Method: 提出一个贝叶斯框架，将宪法定义为评估标准的先验分布P(R)，将评分标准定义为条件实例化R_x~P(R|x)。在此统一视图下，沿着先验-后验轴提出一个评分标准引导RL的分类体系，涵盖宪法AI、实例特定评分标准、过程级监督、自进化评分标准及其智能体和多模态扩展。此外，还进行了语言学分析，研究粒度权衡、语义漂移和语言奖励黑客对对齐可靠性的影响。

Result: 提出了一个统一的贝叶斯理论框架来组织评分标准引导的RL领域，建立了系统性的分类学，并揭示了粒度权衡、语义漂移和语言奖励黑客等关键开放问题。

Conclusion: 评分标准引导的强化学习通过结构化可解释的评估标准弥补了传统RLHF的不足，但仍面临语言相关挑战，未来研究需关注这些开放问题以提升对齐可靠性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has become the dominant paradigm for aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, traditional RLHF relies on scalar reward signals that lack interpretability and fail to capture the multifaceted nature of response quality. Rubric-guided reinforcement learning addresses these limitations by introducing structured, interpretable evaluation criteria, or rubrics, as the backbone of reward design, feedback generation, and policy optimization. In this survey, we introduce a Bayesian framework that defines constitutions as prior distributions $P(R)$ over evaluation criteria and rubrics as conditional instantiations $R_x \sim P(R|x)$. Under this unified view, we present a taxonomy of rubric-guided RL along the prior-posterior axis, covering constitutional AI, instance-specific rubrics, process-level supervision, self-evolving rubrics, and their agentic and multimodal extensions. Furthermore, as rubrics are natural-language artifacts, we present a linguistic analysis of how granularity trade-offs, semantic drift, and linguistic reward hacking impact alignment reliability, identifying key open problems for future research.

</details>


### [87] [How Do Linear Probes Emerge? A Circuit-Tracing Framework with Concept-Targeted Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27510)
*Vedant Palit,Florent Draye,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出概念定向归因（CTA），用于训练与线性探针方向相关的归属图，从而解释内部概念表征为何在提示中出现，而非仅解释下一token预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Transcoder归属图仅用于解释模型为何对某个下一token赋予高概率。然而，模型的内部概念表征（由线性探针检测）与生成token未必直接关联，需要一个框架来解释这些内部概念如何产生，而不仅仅是预测行为。

Method: 使用Cross-Layer Transcoders训练探针定向的归属图，通过图级特征预测探针准确率，利用局部特征识别驱动逐提示分类的稀疏组件，并通过因果消融实验对比探针定向与logit定向图的功能差异。

Result: 探针定向图具有预测性结构：图级特征预测探针准确率（ρ=0.91, R²=0.84）；因果消融表明两种图捕获功能不同的机制——移除探针相关特征降低内部概念分数但保留生成token，而移除logit相关特征在92%-100%情况下改变生成token但对探针分数几乎无影响。

Conclusion: CTA提供了一个从行为探针准确率迈向机制性探针解释的框架，支持对内部概念表征（包括安全关键概念）进行更细粒度的审计。

Abstract: Transcoder attribution graphs are usually trained to explain why a model assigns high probability to a particular next token. We introduce Concept-Targeted Attribution (CTA), which instead trains attribution graphs with respect to a linear probe direction. CTA therefore yields probe-specific circuits that explain why an internal concept representation arises in a prompt, independently of whether it is expressed in the generated token. Using Cross-Layer Transcoders, we show that these probe-targeted graphs contain predictive structure: graph-level features predict probe accuracy across four widely studied concept categories ($ρ= 0.91$, $R^2 = 0.84$), while local features identify the sparse components driving per-prompt classification. This connects probe performance to interpretable circuit structure, allowing us to ask not only whether a probe works, but which internal computations make it work. Causal ablations further show that probe-targeted and logit-targeted graphs capture functionally distinct mechanisms. Removing probe-relevant features reduces internal concept scores while largely preserving generated tokens, whereas removing logit-relevant features changes the generated token in 92% to 100% of cases with near-zero effect on probe scores. CTA provides a framework for moving from behavioral probe accuracy to mechanistic explanations of probe performance, enabling more detailed audits of internal concept representations, including safety-critical ones. Our code is available at https://github.com/vedantpalit/concept-targeted-attribution

</details>


### [88] [Trajectory-Level Speculative Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27514)
*Tianxiang Pan,Baitao Gong,Mo Guang,Hongwei Yong,Tianpeng Jiang,Yaqian Li,Zheng Cao,Kaiwen Long*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向扩散式语言模型（dLLM）的轨迹级投机解码框架，通过置信度分层树探索构建草稿去噪轨迹，并利用双向注意力掩码进行块级并行验证，减少了30-40%的去噪迭代，实现了7-14倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的dLLM解码策略在低置信度下会退化为单token生成，严重限制吞吐量。传统自回归模型的投机解码方法无法直接适用于dLLM，因为dLLM需要对去噪轨迹（多token更新的序列，包含明确位置和去掩码顺序）进行投机，而非简单的左右顺序token序列。

Method: 提出轨迹级投机框架：(1) 通过置信度分层树探索构建草稿去噪轨迹；(2) 利用双向注意力掩码进行块级并行验证；(3) 引入块间投机（inter-block speculation），利用扩散模型的双向结构进行跨块前视。正式刻画了该方法的精确性条件，并识别出轨迹漂移是增加并行性的根本代价。基于Fast-dLLM的双缓存基础设施实现。

Result: 减少30-40%的去噪迭代次数，tokens-per-step从2.6提升至4.3，与原始dLLM相比实现7-14倍加速，与Fast-dLLM相比实现1.3倍加速，在推理和代码基准上准确率变化小于1%。

Conclusion: 轨迹级投机解码有效解决了dLLM在低置信度下并行解码退化为单token生成的问题，通过探索去噪轨迹空间和双向结构利用，在不显著损失准确率的前提下大幅提升dLLM的解码吞吐量。

Abstract: Diffusion-based language models (dLLMs) enable parallel token generation through iterative denoising, but existing decoding strategies collapse to single-token generation under low confidence, severely limiting throughput. Unlike autoregressive models where speculative decoding operates on token sequences in a fixed left-to-right order, dLLMs require speculating over denoising trajectories-sequences of multi-token updates with explicit positions and unmasking orders. We develop a trajectory-level speculative framework that constructs draft denoising trajectories via confidence-stratified tree exploration and verifies them through blockwise parallel evaluation with bidirectional attention masking. Our method further introduces inter-block speculation, exploiting diffusion models' bidirectional structure to perform cross-block lookahead. We formally characterize when this approach is exact and identify trajectory drift as the fundamental cost of increased parallelism. Building on Fast-dLLM's dual-cache infrastructure, our framework reduces denoising iterations by 30-40% and increases tokens-per-step from 2.6 to 4.3, achieving 7-14x speedup over vanilla dLLMs and 1.3x over Fast-dLLM with less than 1% accuracy change across reasoning and code benchmarks.

</details>


### [89] [When Tokenizers Fail: Byte-Level Chunking for Zero-Shot Transfer to Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27658)
*Sanjeev Kumar,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应分层网络框架，通过将字节嵌入直接从冻结子词模型的表示中初始化，并辅以块对齐损失和词性监督，在不进行大量训练的情况下弥合字节级模型与子词模型之间的模态差距，在六种语言的词级形态学任务上取得最高13.3%的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子词分词在低资源语言处理中存在问题，因为其频率模式受主导语言影响；字节级模型虽能绕过这一问题，但在非拉丁文字的词级任务上存在粒度不匹配。现有的分层字节级架构需要大量训练数据，且与冻结的子词语言模型配合时存在表示不对齐问题。

Method: 提出自适应分层网络框架：1）直接从冻结基础模型的子词表示初始化字节嵌入；2）使用块对齐损失将动态分组的字节块投影到预计算的子词目标；3）插入轻量级词性（POS）监督来引导边界检测。

Result: 在六种语言上的实验表明，该方法在词级形态学任务上优于传统分词方法，词性标注任务最高提升13.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法无需大量训练即可弥合字节级与子词级模型之间的表示差距，为低资源语言处理提供了一种有效的免分词方案。

Abstract: Subword tokenization hinders low-resource language processing by imposing frequency patterns from dominant languages onto script-sharing variants. Byte-level models bypass this issue by processing raw UTF-8 characters, yet they create a granularity mismatch for word-level tasks in non-Latin scripts. Hierarchical byte-level architectures address this mismatch by grouping bytes into word-aligned chunks. However, these architectures require massive training data and suffer from representational misalignment when paired with frozen subword-based language models. In this paper, we propose an adapted hierarchical network framework that bridges this modality gap without extensive training. Our method initializes byte embeddings directly from the subword representations of a frozen base model. We apply a chunk alignment loss to project dynamically grouped byte chunks toward precomputed subword targets, and interleave lightweight part-of-speech (POS) supervision to guide boundary detection. Experiments across six languages demonstrate that our tokenizer-free approach improves performance for word-level morphological tasks, yielding up to a 13.3% improvement on POS tagging.

</details>


### [90] [Knowing Before Answering: Decoding Language Models for Reliable RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27661)
*Syed Mahbubul Huq,Christopher Child,Tillman Weyde,Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究RAG系统中的检索信息质量判断问题，提出利用模型内部信号（隐藏激活和注意力特征）训练轻量级线性分类器，将RAG输入分为充分、不充分和冲突三类，在16个语言模型上优于提示词基线和专用RAG模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统中检索提供的信息可能不充分或相互冲突，系统需要能识别这些情况来决定是否作答，而不仅仅是知道何时作答。

Method: 将问题框架化为三分类问题（充分/不充分/冲突），构建包含虚构信息的受控基准数据集，使用模型的隐藏激活和注意力衍生特征作为输入，训练轻量级线性模型进行分类。

Result: 在16个不同架构和规模的语言模型上，基于特征的路由器一致优于提示词基线和专用RAG模型；中间层包含最有信息的分类信号，隐藏激活比注意力值或MLP特征输出更有效。

Conclusion: 语言模型内部编码了检索证据是否足以支持回答的信号，该信号可以被可靠地解码用于RAG分流（triage）。

Abstract: In Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), retrieval may provide insufficient or conflicting information needed to answer a question. The system should not only know when to answer but also be able to identify cases in which the documents provided in RAG are insufficient or contain conflicting information. This can be framed as a three-way classification problem, where we use the model's internal signals to determine whether the provided information in the input can be classified as sufficient, insufficient, or conflicting. We create a controlled benchmark dataset that replicates a RAG setup with fictitious information and labels each instance as answerable, insufficient, or conflicting. We use hidden activations and attention-derived features as inputs to train a lightweight linear model to distinguish among the three classes. Across 16 language models spanning different architectures and a range of model sizes, our feature-based router consistently outperforms prompting-based baselines and the performance of specialised RAG-models. We further conduct analyses into the information dynamics of the models. We show that the most informative signals for the classification are available in the middle layers, with hidden activation states being more effective than attention values or the MLP-feature outputs in most of the tested models. Overall, our results suggest that language models internally encode whether retrieved evidence is sufficient to support answering, and that this signal can be decoded reliably for RAG triage.

</details>


### [91] [First Make It Playable, Then Make It Good: Staged Interaction Learning for Small Dialogue-Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27672)
*Syed Mahbubul Huq,Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Qwen-GuidePlay-2B，一个用于对话游戏交互的20亿参数语言模型，通过三步微调策略（成功轨迹SFT、加权回合级SFT、教师引导SFT）在Playpen基准上取得优异表现，并获得官方挑战赛第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索小型语言模型在对话游戏交互任务中的性能，研究不同的数据筛选和训练策略对小模型决策能力的影响，验证精心设计的训练方案是否能让小模型达到优秀表现。

Method: 基于Qwen3.5-2B进行三步微调：a)仅使用Playpen中成功的游戏轨迹进行SFT；b)加权回合级SFT；c)教师引导SFT（大模型仅用于修复格式和评估样本，不产生新动作标签）。

Result: 在Playpen验证集上取得57.12 clemscore和42.68 statscore；官方挑战赛中clemscore delta排名第二（较基础模型提升约+36）；证明回归完整轨迹有助于可玩性，回合级和教师引导训练提升决策能力。

Conclusion: 小模型通过精心设计的数据筛选和训练策略即可获得良好性能，无需依赖复杂的程序化方法（如replay-repair和hard-example mining），模型和代码均已开源以支持复现。

Abstract: We present Qwen-GuidePlay-2B, a 2B-parameter language model for dialogue-game interaction. We fine-tune Qwen3.5-2B using three steps: a) SFT on only successful game trajectories from Playpen, b) weighted turn-level SFT, and c) teacher-guided SFT. The teacher model (which is a larger model) is only used to fix formatting and evaluate examples, but does not create new gold actions. Our final model scores 57.12 clemscore and 42.68 statscore on the public Playpen validation. In the officially released challenge results, our model obtains the second-highest Playpen clemscore delta among submitted systems (which is approximately +36 over its base model). Our findings suggest that imitating full trajectories helps with playability, while turn-level and teacher-guided training usually improve decision-making and increase the overall score. Alternative procedurally heavy approaches like replay-repair and hard-example mining did not help, which suggests that small models are performant simply by using careful curation strategies rather than aggressive changes. We make available both the model and the code for reproducibility.

</details>


### [92] [Below the Noise Floor: Bimodal Seed Collapse and Distinct Failure Modes in Small-Model Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27729)
*Dipto Sumit,Sakib Ul Haque,Farig Sadeque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究小模型在医疗API函数路由任务上的知识蒸馏问题，发现单次种子评估不可靠：多类KD变体存在双峰崩溃（包括一种新的输出截断模式），种子方差可达48.7个百分点，且跨拆分增益在受控测试下会反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数路由（从固定目录中选择正确的API调用）是部署中常见问题，小模型有吸引力但知识蒸馏的增益通常在单种子、小规模下报告，种子方差未知，导致结论可靠性存疑。

Method: 在740实例的医疗API路由任务上，使用1.5B Qwen学生模型和20B教师模型，比较八种KD变体与监督交叉熵，关键配置使用3到6个种子进行重复实验，评估每种子标准差和崩溃模式。

Result: 发现：(i)每种子标准差高达2.8到48.7个百分点；(ii)七种KD变体中有三种出现双峰崩溃且至少有1/3到1/5种子低于55%准确率，另一种方差升高；(iii)崩溃有不同模式——ce_kd和ce_paraphrase为错误函数选择，reasoning_kd出现未记录的输出截断模式（准确率仅0.9%）；(iv)仅progressive_kd和rank_kd在观测种子下无崩溃（标准差≤3.9个百分点）；(v)跨拆分的+3.78pp增益在受控多种子重测下反转为-2.70pp。

Conclusion: 单种子评估无法检测小模型KD中的关键失败模式，必须采用多种子受控实验来可靠评估KD方法。

Abstract: Function routing -- selecting the correct API call from a fixed catalog given a natural-language request -- is a deployment problem where small students are attractive but knowledge distillation gains are typically reported single-seed, at scales where seed variance is unknown. On a 740-instance healthcare API routing task with a 1.5B Qwen student and a 20B teacher, we compare eight KD variants against supervised cross-entropy, using three to six seeds for key configurations. We find: (i) per-seed standard deviation ranges from 2.8 to 48.7 percentage points, swallowing every claimed KD gain below five points; (ii) three of seven KD variants exhibit bimodal collapse, with at least one in three to five seeds falling below 55% accuracy while the others train normally, and a fourth showing elevated variance; (iii) collapse has distinct modes -- wrong-function selection for ce_kd and ce_paraphrase, and a previously undocumented output-truncation mode for reasoning_kd, where the model emits reasoning but terminates before producing a function name (0.9% accuracy); (iv) only progressive_kd and rank_kd avoid collapse across observed seeds, with sigma <= 3.9 pp; (v) a naive cross-split +3.78 pp gain from input enrichment reverses to -2.70 pp under controlled within-split multi-seed re-testing. Single-seed evaluation is therefore unable to detect central failure modes in small-model KD.

</details>


### [93] [Load-Bearing Context: The Question Damage Score for Evaluating Context Reliance in Linguistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27756)
*Neh Majmudar,Elena Filatova*

Main category: cs.CL
