TL;DR: 本文提出一个诊断框架，通过删除单个上下文示例来评估大型语言模型是从上下文推理还是依赖先验知识，使用语言学奥林匹克谜题作为受控实验环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断LLM的答案来源于上下文还是先验知识是一个基础性难题，需要一个受控环境来隔离和评估这一行为。

Method: 利用53个UK语言学奥林匹克谜题，通过均匀随机删除和基于纠错码启发的定向删除两种方式移除单个上下文示例，引入Question Damage Score形式化评估，并测试三个前沿LLM在信息不足时应弃权的能力。

Result: 前沿LLM在负载关键上下文被移除后很少弃权，常常继续给出正确答案，表明它们依赖先验知识或记忆而非纯粹的上下文推理。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅可用于研究弃权行为，还能支持上下文依赖的细粒度分析，包括因果干预、停止集分析、定向污染研究和机制可解释性。

Abstract: Determining whether large language models derive answers from context or prior knowledge remains a fundamental challenge. Self-contained linguistic olympiad puzzles provide a controlled setting where all answers derive solely from expert-designed context examples without external knowledge. Removing individual context examples can eliminate information needed for specific questions while leaving the rest of the puzzle unchanged. We leverage this to introduce a diagnostic framework for analyzing individual context examples. Using 53 UK Linguistics Olympiad puzzles, we generate two modified variants by deleting a single context example: (1) uniform random deletion, and (2) targeted deletion (inspired by error-correcting codes) to remove a structurally load-bearing example uniquely carrying necessary information. We formalize this impact using a Question Damage Score to classify puzzles as fragile or robust. Evaluating three frontier LLMs under instructions to abstain when information is insufficient, we find they rarely abstain, often continuing to produce correct answers after load-bearing context is removed. These findings motivate further investigation into context-based reasoning, prior knowledge, memorization, and linguistic inference. Beyond abstention, the framework enables fine-grained analyses of context reliance, including causal interventions, stopping-set analysis, targeted contamination studies, and mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [94] [Informational Antilocality and the Locality Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27760)
*Andrew McInnerney,Shane Storks,Steven Abney,Richard L. Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer语言模型学习"k-反局部语言"的能力，发现LLM在不同反局部程度的语言上最终损失相近，但反局部性越强收敛越慢，支持了非局部依赖更难学习的观点，但证据来自学习速度而非最终学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer语言模型在学习非局部依赖方面的内在偏差，即LLM是否真的对局部依赖有天然偏好。

Method: 构造k-反局部语言（任意k个连续符号间无互信息），通过逐步增大k值来增加反局部程度，然后在此类语言上训练LLM，比较不同k值下的交叉熵损失和收敛速度。

Result: LLM在具有不同反局部程度的语言上最终能达到相近的交叉熵损失，但反局部性越强的语言收敛速度越慢。

Conclusion: 非局部依赖确实对LLM的学习更具挑战性，但这种困难体现在学习速度上而非最终学习成果上。

Abstract: We consider the ability of transformer-based language models (LLMs) to learn what we call k-antilocal languages, i.e., languages that have no mutual information across any span of $k$ contiguous symbols. We construct such languages with increasing $k$, finding that LLMs trained on them achieve comparable cross-entropy loss regardless of antilocality, but converge more slowly on more antilocal languages. Our findings support the idea that non-local dependencies are more difficult to learn, but the evidence for this bias comes from learning speed rather than learning success.

</details>


### [95] [Compositional Failure in Audio-Visual LLMs: Late-Layer Prior Dominance Under Cross-modal Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27785)
*Adarsh Sudheer,David Li,Omar Elbanna,Ishaan Kodarapu,Arjun Bahuguna,Vasu Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了音视频大语言模型（AV-LLM）在跨模态冲突场景下的组合泛化能力，发现模型在音频与视频语义不一致时表现出"先验主导"失败模式：模型倾向于输出内部偏好的答案模式而非基于冲突输入进行推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AV-LLM评估多聚焦于一致性的多模态输入，忽略了音视频语义冲突这一更具挑战性的组合泛化场景。作者希望揭示AV-LLM在跨模态冲突下的真实推理能力局限性。

Method: 以音频-视觉冲突作为组合泛化测试，在VideoLLaMA 2-7B-AV和InternVideo2上进行行为评测，并结合机制可解释性分析，考察模型在冲突条件下的层内决策专注度与输出先验偏移。

Result: 三种对齐配置在AVHBench的精确字符串Yes/No子集上接近随机水平，尽管输出先验显著变化；InternVideo2在跨模态冲突下准确率下降32.3%，并伴随17.3%的指令遵循失败。机制分析表明模型的决策承诺集中在约25.5±1层。

Conclusion: AV-LLM存在"先验主导"失败模式——模型在后期层对内部偏好的答案模式做出承诺，而这种承诺对冲突输入的证据支撑薄弱。更强的时间对齐只能改变答案偏差，无法提升组合冲突解决能力。

Abstract: We study audio-visual conflict as a compositional generalization test for AV-LLMs: the model must combine synchronized but semantically incompatible audio and video evidence and decide whether the pair matches. On VideoLLaMA 2-7B-AV, three alignment configurations remain nearchance on the scored exact-string Yes/No subset of AVHBench, even though their output priors shift substantially. Similarly, off-the-shelf InternVideo2 experienced a 32.3% accuracy decrease specifically under cross-modal conflict, accompanied by a 17.3% instruction-following failure. We call this failure mode prior dominance: late-layer commitment to an internally preferred answer pattern that is weakly grounded in the conflicting inputs. To explain this behavior, we conduct a mechanistic interpretability analysis and find that commitment remains concentrated at 25.5 $\pm$ 1 layers. We show that stronger temporal alignment changes answer bias, but do not improve compositional conflict resolution. Code and data to reproduce our mechanistic audit and behavioral evaluations are available at https://github.com/AdarshSudheer09/AVHBench-dmai.

</details>


### [96] [Representation of syntax in LLMs through the lens of linear distance and similarity-aware entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27813)
*Juan Pablo Vigneaux,Mary Kennedy,Khalil Iskarous,Robert Frank,Matilde Marcolli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过引入带标签的无方向依存得分（UASL）来细分结构探针的评价指标，发现不同句法关系的重建准确性存在显著差异，并识别出两个主要预测因素：词汇间的对数线性距离的均值和离散度，以及句法关系中心词的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构探针评估方法仅使用单一的未标记无方向依存得分（UUAS），忽略了不同句法关系之间的差异。作者希望更细致地理解神经语言模型如何表征不同类型的句法关系。

Method: 将原有的UUAS指标细化为按句法关系标签分别计算的UASL，在多种模型规模和架构上进行分析，并通过回归等方法识别预测UASL变异的关键因素：线性距离（对数尺度）的均值与离散度，以及句法关系中心词基于相似性感知熵的多样性。

Result: 研究发现UASL在不同句法关系间存在显著差异，这些差异很大程度上可由上述两个因素预测，且结果在多种模型规模和架构上保持一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明语言模型中句法表征的抽象程度因句法关系而异，且这种表征与嵌入空间的几何属性（如距离和多样性）密切相关，为理解语言模型句法表征的本质提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Structural probes were introduced by Hewitt and Manning to reconstruct syntactic trees from a neural language model's latent representations. They are evaluated by calculating the proportion of syntactic tree edges correctly reconstructed over an annotated corpus (as measured by undirected unlabeled attachment score). Here, we disaggregate this measure, considering undirected attachment score by label (UASL), which assesses the reconstruction accuracy of each syntactic relation separately, establishing important differences among relations that overlap linguistic distinctions. Moreover, we identify two factors that predict most of UASL's variability across relations: (i) the mean and dispersion of the linear distance (on a log scale) between the related words, and (ii) the diversity (similarity-aware entropy) of the syntactic relation's head. These results, which hold across a range of model sizes and architectures, shed light on the degree of abstraction of the representation of syntax in language models and the dependence of such representation on geometric properties of the embedding space.

</details>


### [97] [PersonaEdit: Representative Sample Selection for Personalized Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27816)
*You-Mei Huang,Chung-Chi Chen,An-Zi Yen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonaEdit，一种基于隐藏表征聚类的比例分层采样策略，用于高效选择代表性编辑样本，实现LLM个性化。研究发现模型编辑能有效用于个性化，且与检索增强结合可进一步提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于检索的LLM个性化方法依赖检索质量且在长期交互中性能退化；模型编辑虽在事实知识编辑中有效，但直接扩展到个性化面临计算成本高和编辑间干扰的问题。

Method: 提出PersonaEdit方法，采用隐藏表征聚类策略，通过比例分层采样从大量用户数据中选择代表性编辑样本，从而减少编辑数量并降低计算成本。

Result: 实验表明模型编辑对个性化是有效的；所提出的选择策略在大幅减少编辑样本数量的同时保持了大部分性能；将模型编辑与检索增强结合可进一步提升个性化效果。

Conclusion: 模型编辑是一种高效且可扩展的LLM个性化方法，编辑知识与检索上下文提供互补信息，二者结合效果更佳。

Abstract: Personalization has attracted growing interest in LLM applications, yet existing retrieval-based approaches depend heavily on retrieval quality and degrade in long-term interactions. Model editing, which directly modifies internal model parameters to incorporate new knowledge, has demonstrated effective knowledge modification capabilities in factual knowledge editing tasks and may provide a potential solution for personalization. However, scaling model editing to personalization is non-trivial. Editing large amounts of user data increases computational cost and causes interference among edits, motivating the need for effective sample selection. To address this issue, we propose, PersonaEdit, a hidden representation clustering strategy that selects representative editing samples through proportional stratified sampling. Experiments show that model editing is effective for personalization, and that our selection strategy preserves most of the performance while substantially reducing the number of required editing samples. Beyond standalone editing, we find that combining model editing with retrieval-based prompt augmentation further improves personalization, as edited knowledge and retrieved context provide complementary information. These results demonstrate the potential of model editing as an efficient and scalable approach for LLM personalization.

</details>


### [98] [EvoHarmBench: Breaking Content Moderation with Iterative Human-Like Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27844)
*Ruijie Jian,Benlei Cui,Ting Ma,Haidong Ding,Kangwei Liu,Ziwen Xu,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Ziqiang Zhu,Junjie Li,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EvoHarmBench，首个动态对抗性评估框架，用于测试内容审核系统的鲁棒性。通过迭代优化策略在语义聚类层面演化规避攻击，同时兼顾攻击成功率与人类可读性，实验发现SOTA大模型审核系统在12轮优化后攻击成功率高达80.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害内容检测评估依赖静态基准，无法反映真实平台上用户根据审核反馈持续修改表达方式的对抗性生态系统，导致离线评测分数与在线部署效果存在显著差距。

Method: 构建动态对抗性评估框架EvoHarmBench，采用迭代优化循环在语义聚类层面演化规避策略，同时优化攻击成功率和人类可读性；使用5,002个真实世界对抗样本，覆盖5类违规类别的229个语义子聚类，系统评估基于LLM的防御模型。

Result: 经过十二轮优化迭代，在可读性约束下，针对SOTA大模型审核系统的攻击成功率高达80.3%，即使领先的商业系统也存在显著漏洞。

Conclusion: 呼吁内容安全研究从静态基准评测转向动态对抗性评估，并公开了完整的基准数据、评估框架和代码。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of harmful content detection rely predominantly on static benchmarks, which struggle to reflect the interactive adversarial ecosystem of real-world content platforms where users continuously revise their expressions in response to moderation feedback. This mismatch creates a significant performance gap between offline benchmark scores and online deployment effectiveness. To the best of our knowledge, we present EvoHarmBench, the first dynamic adversarial evaluation framework for content moderation systems. The framework employs an iterative optimization loop that evolves evasion strategies at the semantic-cluster level, while simultaneously optimizing for evasion success and human readability. We systematically evaluate LLM-based defense models which are widely used in real world moderation systems. The evaluation covers 229 semantic sub-clusters across five violation categories, derived from 5,002 real-world adversarial samples collected from content platforms. Our experiments reveal substantial vulnerabilities even in leading commercial systems: after twelve optimization iterations, the attack success rate under readability constraints reaches 80.3% within SOTA LLM moderators. We will release the full benchmark data, evaluation framework, and code to encourage a shift from static benchmarking toward dynamic adversarial evaluation in content safety research.

</details>


### [99] [AI Writers Have a Consistent Stylometric Footprint, but AI Editors Do Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27855)
*Zhengyang Shan,Yukyung Lee,Sophie Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现AI生成和AI编辑文本在文体测量特征上留下不同的痕迹：AI生成文本在熵和词汇多样性上呈现一致性提升，而AI编辑文本仅显示词汇多样性小幅增加和熵下降，词汇密度成为主导信号，说明"AI文本"并非单一现象，应分别研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明AI生成文本在文体测量上与人类写作有区别，但LLM不仅用于生成文本，还越来越多地用于编辑人类写作。此前不清楚AI生成和AI编辑是否留下相同的可检测痕迹，作者希望厘清这一问题。

Method: 作者使用8个LLM和5个领域，对AI生成文本和AI编辑文本进行文体测量特征分析，比较两者相对于人类写作的可分性，并识别主导特征。

Result: AI生成在熵和词汇多样性上形成一致的"文体测量足迹"，可跨模型和领域稳定区分AI生成与人类写作；AI编辑却不会复制这一足迹，仅词汇多样性小幅上升、熵下降，词汇密度成为编辑的主要信号。AI编辑与AI生成可被区分，但与人类写作的区分效果显著减弱。

Conclusion: AI生成与AI编辑在文体测量上留下性质不同的痕迹，应将二者作为独立的现象分别研究，而非统一视为"AI文本"。

Abstract: Text generated by large language models (LLMs) has been shown to be stylometrically distinct from human-written text \citep{andreDetectingAIAuthorship2023, shahDetectingUnmaskingAIGenerated2023, oparaStyloAIDistinguishingAIGenerated2024, soto2024fewshot, liLinguisticDifferencesAI2025, selviogluFeatureExtractionAnalysis2025}. But LLMs are increasingly used not only to generate text but also to edit human writing, and it is unclear whether the two leave the same trace. We show that AI generation leaves a consistent ``stylometric footprint'': a small subset of features, primarily entropy and lexical diversity, consistently separates AI-generated text from human writing across 8 LLMs and 5 domains, while the remaining features depend heavily on the domain and generator. AI editing, however, does not reproduce the same footprint. Relative to their human-written sources, AI-edited texts show only a small increase in lexical diversity and a decrease in entropy, rather than the joint increase that characterizes AI generation. Lexical density, which contributes little to generation, instead becomes the dominant editing-associated signal. Stylometric features therefore separate AI-edited text from AI-generated text but are substantially less effective at separating it from human-written text. Our results suggest that ``AI text'' is not a single phenomenon: generation and editing leave qualitatively different stylometric traces and should be studied separately.

</details>


### [100] [OpenStamp: A Watermark for Open-Source Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27899)
*Miroojin Bakshi,Saksham Rastogi,Danish Pruthi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpenStamp是一种将水印逻辑直接编码到模型权重（仅修改最终投影/unembedding层）的文本水印技术，针对开源大语言模型设计，具有更好的检测性能、更小的模型能力退化，且对改写攻击和微调去除更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于修改token采样概率的水印方法不适用于开源模型，因为用户拥有白盒访问权限，可以轻易在推理过程中禁用水印。因此需要一种能够嵌入到模型权重中的水印方案。

Method: OpenStamp通过仅修改模型的最终投影层（unembedding层）将水印逻辑直接编码到模型权重中，从而在生成过程中无需依赖采样阶段的修改即可实现水印嵌入。

Result: 在两个模型上的实验表明，OpenStamp相比先前方法实现了更优的检测性能，同时对模型能力的退化最小；水印对改写攻击更为鲁棒，且更难通过事后微调被去除。论文还发布了代码和4个流行开源模型的水印版本。

Conclusion: OpenStamp为开源LLM提供了一种重量级的水印方案，通过在模型权重层面嵌入水印，克服了传统采样级水印在开源场景下的局限性。

Abstract: With the growing prevalence of large language model (LLM) generated content, watermarking is considered a promising approach for attributing text to LLMs and distinguishing it from human-written content. A prominent class of techniques embeds subtle but detectable signals in generated text by modifying token sampling probabilities. However, such methods are unsuitable for open-source models, where users have white-box access and can easily disable watermarking during inference. In this work, we introduce OpenStamp, a watermarking technique that encodes the watermarking logic directly into the model weights by modifying only the final projection, or unembedding, layer. Through experiments across two models, we show that OpenStamp achieves superior detection performance, with minimal degradation in model capabilities compared to prior methods. The implanted watermark is explicitly designed, and empirically confirmed, to be more robust to paraphrasing attacks and harder to scrub off through post-hoc fine-tuning than prior open-source watermarks. To enable developers to watermark their models, we release our code alongside watermarked versions of 4 popular open-source models.

</details>


### [101] [LandingAgent: A Reference-Annotated Dataset and Agentic Generation Framework for Landing Pages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27902)
*Injun Baek,HyeongSeok Lee,Yearim Kim,Junhoo Lee,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LandingBench数据集和LandingAgent框架，用于目标接地、参考引导的落地页生成，通过三阶段智能体框架（画像分析、参考引导线框构建、评审优化）生成可执行的网页代码，在保真度、简洁性、可读性、美观度和结构多样性上优于直接提示生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然能根据自然语言提示生成网页代码，但直接生成往往产生模板化页面和缺乏支撑的说服性声明。现有方法缺乏对真实落地页中可复用模式（如章节序列、布局模式、语气描述、视觉强调和CTA结构）的系统化抽象和引导。

Method: 提出LandingBench数据集，将真实落地页抽象为参考配置文件（包含章节序列、布局模式、语气描述、视觉强调和CTA结构）；在此基础上提出LandingAgent，一个三阶段智能体框架：(1) 目标画像构建，(2) 参考引导的线框设计，(3) 通过批评引导的打磨优化；最终生成可执行的落地页代码。

Result: 实验表明，与直接提示生成相比，LandingAgent在目标接地性、呈现质量和结构多样性方面均有提升，同时在保真度、简洁性、可读性和美学方面表现更好。

Conclusion: 通过参考引导的智能体框架可以有效克服LLM直接生成落地页时的模板化和说服力不足问题，实现既有真实页面可复用模式又具目标针对性的高质量落地页生成。

Abstract: Landing pages are goal-oriented web interfaces that must communicate a target-specific value proposition while organizing information flow, visual hierarchy, and calls to action (CTA). Although large language models can generate plausible webpage code from natural-language prompts, direct generation often yields generic templates and unsupported persuasive claims. We study target-grounded, reference-guided landing-page generation, where a system must create an executable page for a new target by adapting reusable patterns from real pages without copying them. We introduce LandingBench, a reference-profile dataset that abstracts real landing pages into section sequences, layout patterns, tone descriptors, visual emphasis, and CTA structure. Building on LandingBench, we propose LandingAgent, a three-phase agentic framework that profiles the target, constructs a reference-guided wireframe, and refines the page through critique-guided polishing. We evaluate LandingAgent against direct prompting on faithfulness, conciseness, readability, aesthetics, and structural diversity. Experiments show improved target grounding, presentation quality, and layout diversity. Code is available at https://github.com/IAURAI/LandingAgent.

</details>


### [102] [What Makes Agent Memory Useful for Reliable Unanswerable Question Handling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27924)
*Chuanyuan Tan,Junjie Yu,Yuxin Wang,Yining Zheng,Xipeng Qiu,Wenliang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于LLM的代理人记忆在不可回答问题（UAQ）处理中的作用，在统一agentic RAG框架下评估四种记忆方法。发现记忆对UAQ的提升是选择性的而非普适的，跨模型记忆复用优于跨数据集迁移，程序性和规则性记忆最为可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠处理不可回答问题（UAQs）对于基于LLM的智能体至关重要，尽管记忆在智能体系统中被广泛使用，但其在UAQ处理中的可靠作用尚不明确。

Method: 在统一的agentic RAG框架下，系统评估了四种代表性记忆方法，覆盖三个UAQ相关数据集和两个基础模型。分析了记忆在不同设置下的表现，包括跨数据集迁移和跨模型复用情况，并考察了决策引导与轨迹塑造、不同记忆表示形式的效果差异。

Result: 记忆在某些设置下能提升UAQ性能，但增益具有选择性且脆弱。跨模型记忆复用比跨数据集迁移更可行，UAQ增益更多通过决策引导而非轨迹塑造得到保持。程序性和规则性记忆最可靠，与互补行为信号结合时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 可靠的UAQ记忆不依赖于存储更多的经验量，而更依赖于保留可迁移的行为引导。程序性指导与行为信号的组合是记忆组成最有效的形式。

Abstract: Reliable handling of unanswerable questions (UAQs) is critical for trustworthy LLM-based agents. Although memory is widely used in agent systems, its role in reliable UAQ handling remains unclear. We present a systematic study of agent memory for UAQ handling under a unified agentic RAG framework, evaluating four representative memory methods across three UAQ-related datasets and two base models.
  We find that memory can improve UAQ performance in some settings, but such gains are selective rather than universal and remain fragile under dataset shift. Interestingly, cross-model memory reuse is often more feasible than cross-dataset transfer, suggesting that shifts in answerability patterns pose a greater challenge to memory reuse than changes in the base model itself. We further find that UAQ gains are more strongly preserved through decision guidance than through trajectory shaping, and that memory effectiveness depends strongly on representation. In particular, procedural and rule-based memories often provide the most reliable support for UAQ handling, while memory composition is most effective when procedural guidance is combined with complementary behavioral signals. Overall, our findings suggest that reliable UAQ memory depends less on storing larger amounts of experience and more on preserving transferable behavioral guidance.

</details>


### [103] [Entity-Memory Graph Retrieval Improves Evidence Coverage in Long-Conversation Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27925)
*Shumao Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Entity-Memory图检索在对话记忆任务中的效果，通过图结构检索与密集余弦排序的对照实验，发现图检索显著提升证据召回率（top-k 25从79.75%提升至84.48%），但未发现最终答案F1分数的显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在对话记忆检索中，利用实体-记忆图结构（结合实体门控、语义融合、时间邻接恢复和密集回填）是否能超越传统密集余弦排序方法，从而提升检索覆盖率和最终答案质量。

Method: 提出Entity-Memory图检索方法：将对话轮次作为Memory节点，通过共享实体连接重复提及，并以有向时间边连接相邻记忆；查询时依次进行实体门控、语义融合、一跳时间恢复和密集回填。实验采用匹配的密集对照以隔离图结构变量，在10个LoCoMo对话的1,986个问题上使用GPT-3.5和DeepSeek提取器验证。

Result: 图检索将官方证据召回率从79.75%提升至84.48%（top-k 25），且从top-k 5到50均有优势；但所有匹配截止点均未支持最终答案F1存在显著差异，嵌入鲁棒性测试中召回指标对嵌入伪影敏感而F1无显著对比。

Conclusion: 图结构带来了检索覆盖率的提升，但并未建立最终答案F1的增益、模型或嵌入等价性，也未验证跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Entity-Memory graph retrieval keeps dialogue turns as verbatim Memory nodes, links repeated mentions through shared Entities, and connects adjacent Memories with directed chronological edges. At query time the retriever moves from Entity gating through semantic fusion and one-hop chronological recovery to dense backfill. The path can keep a neighboring Memory that dense cosine ranking would otherwise omit. A matched dense control shares the Memory and query vectors, context budget, requested answer protocol, and evaluator, isolating graph structure from changes to the reader.
  On 1,986 questions from ten LoCoMo conversations, graph retrieval raises official evidence recall at top-k 25 from 79.7468% to 84.4842%. The recall advantage is supported from top-k 5 to 50, while no matched cutoff supports an overall final-answer F1 difference. Four paper-eligible requested configurations support empirical robustness across the tested GPT-3.5 and DeepSeek extractors on both outcomes. Embedding robustness is mixed: F1 has no supported contrast, but recall is sensitive to the embedding artifact. The comparison isolates a retrieval-coverage gain from graph structure. It does not establish a final-answer F1 gain, model or embedding equivalence, or cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [104] [Lexically conditioned realization ambiguity in Korean predicate morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27966)
*Wonjun Oh,KyungTae Lim,Jungyeul Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨韩语表层实现与形态分析的差异，发现仅凭规范语素序列和语法类别标签无法唯一确定表层形式，特定谓语需依赖词汇身份和实现类别才能确定表层输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨韩语中表层形式是否由规范语素和语法类别唯一确定，揭示纯形态表征的局限性。

Method: 分析韩语中规则与不规则（digeut、bieup、reu、reo）屈折对之间的同音异义现象，论证表层实现需依赖词汇身份与实现类别。

Result: 发现形式相同或近似的词干-词尾配置在不同词汇身份和实现类别下产生不同输出，表层形式并非仅由形态表征决定。

Conclusion: 韩语表层实现揭示了纯形态表征的极限：词汇意义、次范畴化和语义角色结构共同决定谓语身份，进而决定实现类别和表层形式。

Abstract: This paper examines Korean surface realization as distinct from morphological analysis. It asks whether a sequence of canonical morphemes and grammatical category labels uniquely determines the corresponding surface form. The answer is negative for a restricted but theoretically revealing class of Korean predicates. In these cases, formally identical or near-identical stem-ending configurations yield different outputs depending on lexical identity and realization class membership. We analyze this phenomenon as homonymy with inflectional divergence, focusing on regular versus digeut irregular pairs, regular versus bieup irregular pairs, and reu irregular versus reo irregular pairs. These cases show that stem shape and ending alone do not always determine surface realization. Instead, lexical meaning, subcategorization, and semantic role structure help identify the intended predicate; the predicate determines the realization class; and the realization class determines the surface form. Korean realization thus reveals a limit of bare morphological representation.

</details>


### [105] [QUORUM: QUality-Optimized Routing Using Multiple annotators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27974)
*Antonio Purificato,Maria Sofia Bucarelli,Andrea Bacciu,Amin Mantrach,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QUORUM是一个预算感知的路由框架，动态地将每个标注实例分配给人类或LLM标注者，通过基于特征信号估计实例难度，并支持多次标注通过一致性奖励提升可靠性，在多种标注任务中最高提升34.4%标注质量并降低8.8%成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据标注是NLP的核心瓶颈，LLM虽然提供快速廉价的替代方案，但其可靠性高度依赖实例——简单输入表现良好，复杂推理和上下文理解场景常常失败。现有方法依赖模型置信度或不确定性估计，不够可靠，需要更有效的实例分配策略。

Method: QUORUM框架基于特征信号（而非模型置信度）估计实例难度，在固定预算下将每个实例动态路由给人类或LLM标注者；支持单实例多标注，通过一致性奖励机制组合多次标注结果以提升可靠性。

Result: 在闭式和开放式标注任务（含英语和多语言场景）上，QUORUM相比竞品方法最高提升34.4%的标注质量，同时降低8.8%的成本。

Conclusion: QUORUM证明基于特征的难度估计和一致性情奖励的多标注路由策略能有效平衡标注质量与成本，为混合人机标注提供了一个实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Data annotation remains a central bottleneck in natural language processing, requiring human effort to obtain high-quality labels at scale. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a fast and cost-effective alternative, their reliability is highly instance-dependent: they perform well on simple inputs but often fail on examples requiring nuanced reasoning or contextual understanding. In this work, we address this challenge with QUORUM (QUality-Optimized Routing Using Multiple annotators), a budget-aware routing framework that dynamically assigns each instance to human or LLM annotators under a fixed annotation budget. Unlike prior approaches relying on model confidence or uncertainty estimates, QUORUM leverages feature-based signals to estimate instance difficulty and supports multiple annotations per instance, combining them through agreement-based rewards to improve reliability. We evaluate QUORUM across diverse closed- and open-ended annotation tasks in English and multilingual settings, and QUORUM improves annotation quality by up to 34.4% while reducing costs by 8.8% over competing methods. Code can be found at https://github.com/amazon-science/QUORUM.

</details>


### [106] [Predicting Turn-Taking Outcomes in Multi-Party Conversation: Interpretable Modelling of Speech and Gaze Dynamics with Interpersonal Closeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27988)
*Mark Dourado,Karim Haddad,Henrik G. Hassager,Stefania Serafin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用GaMMA语料库，通过可解释的逻辑回归模型，结合目光注视、语音响度和人际亲密感等特征，预测四人自由对话中话轮转换的结果（间隔或重叠），结果显示目光特征与响度结合后模型性能最佳（ROC AUC = 0.76 ± 0.04），且在噪声条件下依然稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流畅的说话者转换是有效对话的基础，取决于对话者预测何时进入对话的能力，而在嘈杂、自然、多方参与的对话环境中，这一过程更加复杂。现有研究缺乏对多人在场情况下话轮转换信号的系统建模。

Method: 使用GaMMA语料库中四人自由对话数据，提取话轮转换前的可解释行为特征（包括目光转移模式、行为对比、熵、基于目光的受话者身份、相互注视、说话者响度及感知人际亲密度IOS），训练逻辑回归模型分类话轮转换结果为间隔或重叠。

Result: 目光特征能捕捉预测性结构，与响度结合后性能提升（ROC AUC = 0.76 ± 0.04）；响度反映说话者控制力，目光分散和指向反映听众准备状态与竞争性进入；模型性能在噪声条件下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 目光注视是对话轮转换动态的补充性、抗噪声线索，结合言语和非言语特征可以有效预测话轮转换结果。

Abstract: Smooth speaker transitions are fundamental to effective conversation and rely on an interlocutor's ability to predict when to enter the conversation. This ability depends on accurately interpreting and expressing the verbal and non-verbal cues that signal when a speaker wishes to take or relinquish the floor. The process becomes even more complex in noisy, natural, multi-party settings, with multiple interlocutors available. This study models how gaze and speech, together with perceived interpersonal closeness, signal conversational floor changes in free four-person dialogue. Using the GaMMA corpus, we trained logistic regression models using interpretable, behaviourally motivated features extracted before each turn-taking event to classify floor-transfer outcomes as gaps or overlaps. Predictors included gaze features such as transition motifs and behavioural contrasts, entropy, gaze-based addressee identity, and mutual gaze, alongside speech features derived from speaker loudness, as well as perceived interpersonal closeness (IOS) between speakers. Results show that gaze features capture predictive structure, and that combining them with loudness improves performance (ROC AUC = 0.76 +- 0.04). Loudness reflected speaker control, while gaze dispersion and addressing indexed listener readiness and competitive entry. Performance remained robust across noise conditions, indicating that gaze provides a complementary, noise-resilient cue to turn-taking dynamics.

</details>


### [107] [Beyond Global Scalars: Synergizing Token-Level Statistics and Deep Semantics for Adversarial AIGC Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28009)
*Peiming Li,Yifan Wang,Zhiyuan Hu,Shiyu Li,Zheng Wei,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC对抗基准（16000样本，覆盖全粒度攻击）和NeuroStat检测框架，通过融合token级概率logits与语义隐藏状态，利用宏状态残差调制自适应校准局部卷积特征，显著提升机器生成文本检测在对抗场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器生成文本检测方法分为训练无关（基于困惑度等全局统计标量）和训练相关（基于语义隐藏状态）两条独立路径，但在对抗场景下均存在根本性缺陷：全局标量是损失性压缩，掩盖了交错文本中的局部概率突发性；纯语义模型对特定指纹过拟合，容易受到欺骗攻击。

Method: 提出神经统计框架NeuroStat，从单一因果语言模型骨干中同时提取未压缩的token级概率logits和深层语义隐藏状态，通过宏状态残差调制融合异构信号，用全局不确定性指标自适应校准局部卷积特征，并采用正交和对比损失确保互补表示的学习。

Result: 在MOSAIC基准上，NeuroStat相比最先进方法表现出极强的鲁棒性，而现有SOTA方法则出现严重性能退化，建立了对抗文本检测的新标准。

Conclusion: 统计信号与语义信号的互补融合是提升机器生成文本检测在对抗攻击下鲁棒性的有效途径，为后续研究提供了新基准和新框架。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of large language models necessitates robust machine-generated text detection. Existing paradigms typically follow two isolated tracks. Training-free methods rely on global statistical scalars such as perplexity, while training-based methods utilize semantic hidden states. Both approaches exhibit fundamental vulnerabilities in adversarial scenarios. Global scalars act as lossy compressions that obscure local probabilistic burstiness in interleaved texts, whereas pure semantic models overfit to specific fingerprints and remain susceptible to spoofing. To expose these flaws, we introduce MOSAIC, a comprehensive adversarial benchmark comprising 16000 samples across a full-granularity attack spectrum. To address these challenges, we propose NeuroStat, an end-to-end framework bridging the statistical and semantic gap. NeuroStat captures uncompressed token-level probabilistic logits alongside deep semantic hidden states from a single causal language model backbone. We fuse these heterogeneous signals through Macro-State Residual Modulation, which adaptively calibrates local convolutional features using global uncertainty indicators. Orthogonal and contrastive losses further ensure the learning of complementary representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeuroStat maintains exceptional robustness on MOSAIC compared to the severe degradation of state-of-the-art methods, establishing a new standard for adversarial text detection. Code and the MOSAIC benchmark are available at https://github.com/TencentBAC/NeuroStat.

</details>


### [108] [Twin Worlds: Equivariance-Based Abstention for Evidence-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28018)
*Vy Nguyen,Ziqi Xu,Jeffrey Chan,Estrid He,Feng Xia,Renqiang Luo,Erik Cambria,Xiuzhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Twin Worlds框架，通过等变性（equivariance）而非不变性（invariance）来检测LLM在知识密集型推理中是否真正基于给定证据进行推理，并在证据不足时触发弃权（abstention）。该方法通过类型化实体替换构建多个"世界"，利用等变性违反作为弃权信号，在四个基准和三个模型上优于现有的不确定性和充分性基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弃权方法依赖不确定性估计或证据充分性检查，但都无法测试推理过程是否真正基于证据。一个关键因素是上下文中实体提及会激活模型记忆中的关联，导致模型生成看似合理但未被证据支持的答案，因此需要一种能够检测推理过程是否真正基于证据的方法。

Method: 提出Twin Worlds框架，采用基于等变性的弃权机制：不同于不变性要求输出保持不变，等变性要求输出在实体替换下相应地变换。通过对原始输入进行类型化实体替换（保持关系结构、减少参数先验），构建多个"世界"，将等变性违反作为弃权信号来识别答案是否可靠地基于给定证据。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和三个模型骨干上，Twin Worlds能够识别答案何时没有可靠地基于给定证据进行推理，并在弃权性能上优于基于不确定性和基于充分性的基线方法。

Conclusion: Twin Worlds通过等变性框架有效提升了LLM在知识密集型推理中的可靠性，为检测推理过程是否真正基于证据提供了新思路。

Abstract: Knowledge-intensive reasoning requires Large Language Models (LLMs) to ground answers in provided evidence. When evidence is insufficient, it is desirable that models abstain rather than confidently generating unsupported answers. Existing abstention methods rely on uncertainty estimation or evidence sufficiency checks, but neither tests whether the reasoning process for generation, driven by the interaction of provided evidence and the model's internal memory parameters, is actually grounded in the evidence. A key contributing factor is that entity mentions in context activate memorised associations, causing models to generate plausible responses ungrounded in evidence. We propose Twin Worlds (TW), a framework for improving reliability in knowledge-intensive reasoning through equivariance-based abstention: unlike invariance, which requires outputs to remain unchanged, equivariance requires outputs to transform correspondingly under entity substitutions. A model grounded in the evidence should produce answers that shift consistently when entities are substituted while their relations are preserved. TW constructs multiple worlds via typed substitutions of the original input that preserve relational structure while reducing parametric priors, and uses equivariance violations as an abstention signal. Across four benchmarks and three model backbones, TW identifies when answers are not reliably grounded in the provided evidence and outperforms uncertainty- and sufficiency-based baselines.

</details>


### [109] [A Shaky Voice Is Not Always a Dodge: Benchmarking Textual and Vocal Evasion Detection in Earnings Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28040)
*Mirae Kim,Seonghun Jeong,Youngjun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DualEvasion基准，用于在财报电话会议中联合检测文本和音频两个维度的规避行为（文本规避与声音信心度），发现现有SOTA多模态模型在检测声音信心度（尤其是不自信回应）方面表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规避检测方法仅关注文本转录，将规避视为单一维度现象。但口语交流中规避本质上具有多维性，说话方式（音频）与说话内容（文本）携带独立且互补的信息，需要联合研究。

Method: 构建DualEvasion基准，包含来自60场财报电话会议的505个标注问答对，每个样本有两个独立标签：文本规避（直接vs.回避）和声音线索（自信vs.不自信）。采用SOTA多模态模型进行实验，并比较不同建模策略（如提供说话者级参考）的效果。

Result: 多模态模型难以检测声音信心度，尤其在不自信回应上表现更差。分析表明模型倾向于孤立地解读声学线索，而非相对于每个说话者的基线来判断。提供说话者级参考仅有适度改进，与人类表现仍有较大差距。

Conclusion: 当前多模态模型在联合检测语音规避多维度信号方面仍能力不足，需要更有效的说话者级声学建模方法，以缩小与人类表现之间的差距。

Abstract: Existing approaches to evasion detection in earnings calls focus on textual transcripts, treating evasion as a single-dimensional phenomenon. We argue that evasion in spoken communication is inherently multidimensional: beyond what executives say, how they say it carries independent and complementary information. To study these dimensions jointly, we introduce DualEvasion, a benchmark for evasion detection across text and audio in earnings call Q&A. The benchmark contains 505 annotated question-answer pairs from 60 earnings calls, each with two independent labels: textual evasion (direct vs. evasive) and vocal cues operationalized as speaker confidence (confident vs. unconfident). Our experiments show that state-of-the-art multimodal models struggle to detect vocal confidence, particularly on unconfident responses. Our analysis suggests these models interpret acoustic cues in isolation rather than relative to each speaker's baseline. Providing speaker-level references yields modest improvements, but a substantial gap with human performance remains.

</details>


### [110] [SimpCue: Cue-Based Prompting for Multilingual Text Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28042)
*Mehrzad Tareh,Horacio Saggion,Stefan Bott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语和意大利语的多语言Easy-to-Read文本简化任务中，向提示中添加显式语言学线索（如句子复杂性信息）是否能改进大语言模型的输出质量。实验使用Qwen3-8B模型，比较基线提示、黄金线索提示和预测线索提示三种设置，发现预测线索提示在SARI、BLEU、chrF和BERTScore四项指标上总体最优，但收益较小且存在语言依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大语言模型可以通过提示（prompting）进行文本简化，但目前尚不清楚在提示中显式加入关于句子复杂性的语言学信息是否有助于提升简化输出的质量。作者希望探究这一问题，以指导多语言Easy-to-Read简化任务的提示设计。

Method: 使用Qwen3-8B模型，在加泰罗尼亚语、西班牙语和意大利语三种语言的句子级Easy-to-Read简化任务中，对比三种提示策略：基线提示（无语言学线索）、黄金线索提示（包含人工标注的金标准语言学线索）和预测线索提示（包含自动预测的语言学线索）。使用SARI、BLEU、chrF和BERTScore四项自动指标进行评估，并辅以人工定性分析。

Result: 预测线索提示在四项自动指标上总体得分最高，但相对于基线的提升幅度较小。黄金线索提示并未在基线上实现一致的改进，且结果因语言而异。表明基于线索的提示可以影响多语言Easy-to-Read简化，但其收益在指标维度和语言维度上都较为有限。

Conclusion: 语言学线索提示对多语言Easy-to-Read文本简化有一定促进作用，尤其是自动预测的线索；但总体效果温和，且高度依赖评估指标和具体语言，尚需进一步研究以优化线索提示策略。

Abstract: Text simplification aims to make complex texts easier to understand while preserving their original meaning. Recent large language models can perform simplification through prompting, but it remains unclear whether adding explicit linguistic information about sentence complexity to the prompt improves their outputs. We investigate this question for multilingual sentence-level Easy-to-Read simplification in Catalan, Spanish, and Italian. Using Qwen3-8B, we compare a baseline prompt, a gold-cue prompt enriched with gold linguistic cues, and a predicted-cue prompt enriched with automatically predicted cues. We evaluate the outputs using SARI, BLEU, chrF, and BERTScore, and complement this evaluation with a manual qualitative analysis. Predicted-cue prompting obtains the best overall scores across all four metrics, although the gains over the baseline are small. Gold-cue prompting does not consistently improve over the baseline, and results vary across languages. These findings indicate that cue-based prompting can influence multilingual Easy-to-Read simplification, but its benefits are modest, metric-dependent, and language-dependent.

</details>


### [111] [CNeo-Bench: Diagnosing Large Language Models on Chinese Neologisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28053)
*Kaiyan Zhao,Zhongtao Miao,Zheyong Xie,Shaosheng Cao,Yoshimasa Tsuruoka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CNeo-Bench，一个包含4,759个中文新词及其参考定义的基准数据集，并配套两层评估框架来区分模型描述新词的能力和操作其底层语言机制的能力。对18个LLM的评估显示中文新词仍是一个开放性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文新词利用了多种独特且罕见的语言机制（如谐音替换、字形拆分），这些机制在其他语言中很少见。现有基准缺乏对这一领域系统性的评估，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 构建CNeo-Bench基准，包含4,759个中文新词，按语言机制分为五个顶层类别和九个次类别；设计两层评估框架，第一层测试模型能否描述新词含义，第二层测试模型能否基于其底层机制进行操作（如源形式恢复）；评估18个LLM并进行了少量样本（few-shot）分析。

Result: 大多数模型在定义生成任务上得分低于40%；多个子类别出现系统的"识别-操作差距"：模型能正确描述新词，但在源形式恢复任务中用语义等价词替换源形式；少量样本提示虽能解决许多困难案例，但仍有相当比例的错误无法通过提示解决。

Conclusion: 中文新词理解对现有LLM仍是开放挑战，部分难题超出提示工程可解决范围，需要更深层的模型能力提升。

Abstract: Chinese neologisms exploit diverse and unique linguistic mechanisms, such as phonetic substitution (e.g., 886 for ``bye-bye'') and visual character decomposition that are rare in other languages. We introduce CNeo-Bench, a benchmark of 4,759 such neologisms with reference definitions, organized into five top-level categories and nine subcategories by the linguistic mechanism behind each expression. CNeo-Bench is paired with a two-tier evaluation framework that separates whether a model can describe a neologism from whether it can operate on its underlying mechanism. Evaluating 18 LLMs, we find that Chinese neologisms remain an open challenge; most models fall below 40\% on definition generation, and on several subcategories a systematic recognition-manipulation gap emerges: models describe neologisms correctly but, in source-form restoration tasks, substitute a semantic equivalent (paraphrase) for the source form rather than producing the source form itself. A few-shot analysis on 1,058 hard items shows that in-context examples can solve many difficult cases, but leave a noticeable portion of errors remaining, indicating challenges beyond prompting alone can address.

</details>


### [112] [H-Scale: Hessian-Guided Scale Refinement for NVFP4 Sub-Byte LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28113)
*Hao Yu,Zheng Li,Dayiheng Liu,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出H-Scale，一种针对NVIDIA Blackwell架构NVFP4格式的轻量级后处理量化方法，通过对角二阶代理优化逐组缩放因子选择，在不增加推理开销的前提下提升LLM量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVFP4格式采用微块设计（如组大小16），提供了灵活的表示能力来捕获局部权重分布和隔离异常值，但也引入了大量且高度敏感的逐组缩放因子搜索空间。现有PTQ方法主要关注量化权重值的精炼，而忽略了缩放因子选择这一关键步骤。

Method: H-Scale作为RTN风格缩放因子选择的即插即用替代方案，不直接最小化权重重建误差，而是利用校准激活推导出对角二阶代理，更直接地针对层输出扰动进行硬件有效的组缩放因子选择。仅需适度的离线校准，推理时零额外开销。

Result: 在固定评估协议下，对主流LLM的实验表明，H-Scale普遍改善了广泛的NVFP4基线方法性能，并使若干变体更接近BF16参考精度。

Conclusion: H-Scale有效地填补了NVFP4 PTQ中缩放因子选择未被充分探索的空白，为Blackwell架构上的LLM推理加速提供了一种轻量且有效的量化精度提升方案。

Abstract: The NVIDIA Blackwell architecture, with native support for the ultra-fine-grained NVFP4 format, opens new opportunities for accelerating large language model (LLM) inference. NVFP4's micro-block design, such as a group size of 16, offers strong representational flexibility for capturing local weight distributions and isolating outliers, but it also introduces a large and highly sensitive space of per-group scaling factors. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods primarily focus on refining quantized weight values, leaving this scale-selection step underexplored. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{H-Scale}, a lightweight post-processing method for NVFP4 per-group scale refinement. Instead of minimizing plain weight reconstruction error, H-Scale selects hardware-valid group scales using a diagonal second-order proxy derived from calibration activations, thereby targeting layer output perturbation more directly. It is designed as a drop-in replacement for RTN-style scale selection in diverse NVFP4 pipelines, requires only modest offline calibration, and introduces strictly zero overhead at inference time. Under a fixed evaluation protocol, experiments on mainstream LLMs show that H-Scale generally improves a broad range of NVFP4 baselines and brings several variants closer to the BF16 reference.

</details>


### [113] [Nested Byte-Level Vocabularies Are Cheap to Deploy and Expensive to Share: A Pre-Registered Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28151)
*Christos Koutsiaris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究字节级BPE分词器的前缀嵌套特性，允许单一语言模型通过切片嵌入和输出头在不同词表大小下运行，实验表明切片在数值上精确且节省66%权重，但共享模型性能低于固定大小专用模型，不过多粒度训练显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用BPE分词器的前缀嵌套特性，使一个语言模型能够支持多种词表大小，从而在部署时灵活权衡模型大小和性能，而无需为每个词表大小单独训练模型。

Method: 利用字节级BPE分词器的前缀嵌套特性，训练共享模型并引入控制token指示活跃词表大小，通过切片嵌入和输出头在不同词表大小下部署；进行了预注册的五个声明验证，训练了30个模型，并设计了2x2消融实验分离控制token与输出限制的影响。

Result: 切片模型在76项检查中逐位复现受限完整模型的logits，减少66%部署权重且不改变延迟；但共享模型在32k和8k词表下分别落后专用模型3.64%和2.96%（超过预设边距）；控制token影响极小且置信区间过零，输出限制成本为0.47%-1.19%；多粒度训练在噪声下鲁棒性提升12.5-15.4点。

Conclusion: 虽然多容量训练在纯性能上不如固定大小专用模型，但显著提升了鲁棒性，且该收益源于多粒度训练本身而非条件控制机制；每个容量对应的性能惩罚与其训练行占比相关，为未来研究提供了可证伪的预测。

Abstract: A byte-level BPE tokenizer is an ordered list of merge rules, so applying only a prefix yields a vocabulary whose token identifiers are the first rows of the full vocabulary. This prefix nesting allows one language model to operate at several vocabulary sizes, use a control token to indicate the active size, and be deployed at any trained size by slicing its embedding and output head. We pre-registered five claims, including margins, seeds, contrasts, and a stop rule, and trained 30 models with 3.1M- and 10.6M-parameter bodies on 200M tokens each. Slicing is numerically exact: across 76 checks, a sliced model reproduces the restricted full model's logits bit for bit and removes 66% of deployed weights without changing latency. However, the shared model trails a fixed-cap specialist by 3.64% bits per byte at 32k against a 1% margin, and by 2.96% at 8k against a 2% margin. A 2x2 ablation separating the control token from output restriction finds that the token changes performance by +0.07% to +0.13%, with all intervals crossing zero, while output restriction costs +0.47% to +1.19%; the factors are substitutes rather than complements. Multi-cap training nevertheless improves robustness: under typographical noise, the same checkpoint degrades 12.5--15.4 points less in its fine mode and outperforms each fixed-cap specialist at that specialist's vocabulary size. A control with neither cap token nor output restriction is equally robust, attributing this benefit to multi-granularity training rather than conditioning. The per-cap penalty tracks each cap's share of training rows, yielding a falsifiable prediction for future work.

</details>


### [114] [FinExam-10K: When Retrieval Helps Financial Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28155)
*Yan Lin,Jingyu Sun,Zhongliang Guo,Qing Li,Zhuohan Xie,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FinExam-10K，一个包含10,198道专家重标注问题的金融专业考试基准（涵盖CFA Level I-III和FRM Part I-II），并引入全覆盖率与上下文完整推理双轨评估体系。实验表明最佳模型准确率为85.29%，但在困难的Hard子集上表现较差；Function-RAG等方法虽然能纠正部分错误但净收益有限，而基于门控机制的FunctionGraph-RAG在保留测试集上实现了小幅但显著的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业金融考试要求模型同时具备领域知识、计算能力和判断力，但目前没有任何基准能在统一协议下完整覆盖CFA和FRM的全部结构，缺乏全面的评估体系。

Method: 构建了FinExam-10K基准数据集（10,198道专家重标注问题），分为Full-Coverage Track和Context-Complete Reasoning Track双轨评估；同时提出基于门控机制的FunctionGraph-RAG方法，仅利用公开数据训练门控模型决定是否调用RAG。

Result: 17个模型中最佳整体准确率为85.29%；Hard子集上最佳分数为Full-Coverage Track 34.68%和上下文完整项54.57%；17个模型共有47个失败项；门控在5,088项保留集上对7.9%的问题调用FunctionGraph-RAG，准确率从70.83%提升到71.23%（p=.0446）。

Conclusion: 现有模型在专业金融推理任务上仍有显著不足，特别是在困难子集上。RAG后处理方法虽然能纠正部分错误但可能带来负面影响，需要更精细的决策机制来平衡收益。

Abstract: Professional financial examinations require models to combine domain knowledge, calculation, and judgment, yet no benchmark covers the full CFA and FRM structure under one protocol. We introduce FinExam-10K, to our knowledge the largest reported English benchmark for this setting, with 10,198 expert-reannotated questions spanning CFA Levels I-III and FRM Parts I-II. We release 5,110 questions and sequester 5,088 for a quarterly maintained leaderboard. To separate coverage from local answerability, we report a 10,198-item Full-Coverage Track and a 7,625-item Context-Complete Reasoning Track, which is the primary basis for claims about reasoning from the supplied record. Across 17 models, the best accuracy is 85.29% overall. On the frozen Hard band, the best score is 34.68% on the Full-Coverage Track and 54.57% on the 372 context-complete items. All 17 models share 47 context-complete failures. Function-RAG and FunctionGraph-RAG rescue hundreds of errors but also overturn many correct answers, producing little or negative net gain. A gate trained only on public data decides from the question and initial response when FunctionGraph-RAG should run. On the 5,088 held-out items, the gate invokes FunctionGraph-RAG for 7.9% of questions and improves accuracy from 70.83% to 71.23% (p = .0446).

</details>


### [115] [Text Restoration of Ancient Documents with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28170)
*Shibingfeng Zhang,Edoardo Caraffa,Annafelicia Zuffrano,Maddalena Modesti,Giovanni Colavizza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究使用语言模型恢复受损古代手稿中因物理缺失（lacunae）导致的文本空缺，提出多种场景和解码策略，发现该任务不能完全自动化但可作为古文字学家的辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古代手稿常因物理损伤导致文本缺失，传统上依赖古文字学家手工复原，本文首次系统研究语言模型在此任务中的可行性。

Method: 提出多种模拟真实条件的场景，根据不同场景选用不同架构的语言模型，并提出若干解码策略以解决缺失边界与模型分词方案不匹配的问题。

Result: 文本恢复无法完全自动化，但可作为辅助工具。模型性能因需恢复的文档结构部分以及缺失文本字符长度是否已知而有显著差异。

Conclusion: 这是首次系统分析语言模型在公式化和非公式化内容上的表现及缺失长度感知对恢复效果的影响，为开发辅助古文字学家的工具提供了指导。

Abstract: Purpose - This study investigates the feasibility of restoring missing text caused by physical lacunae in damaged ancient manuscripts using language models.
  Methodology - The study proposes different scenarios to replicate real-world conditions. Language models of different architectures are applied according to their suitability to each scenario. We also propose several decoding strategies that further enhance performance and address the discrepancy between lacuna boundaries and the models' tokenization schemes.
  Findings - The results reveal that text restoration of these documents cannot be fully automated, but it can serve as a useful tool to assist paleographers in their work. Model performance varies greatly depending on which structural part of the document needs to be restored and whether the character length of missing text is available.
  Originality - This is the first study and to analyze model performance on formulaic and non-formulaic content and the impact of lacuna length awareness in manuscript restoration. Both are recurring challenges in paleographers' manual restoration work. Through systematic comparison and both qualitative and quantitative analysis of different models' performance under varying settings, this study offers a guideline for developing assistive tools to support paleographers.

</details>


### [116] [Embedding Models for Stance-Aware Argument Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28283)
*Angelo Sparacino,Francesca Toni,Adam Dejl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了密集嵌入模型在论点检索中如何同时评估主题相关性和立场（支持/反对）的问题，发现现有模型存在对主题重叠的偏见而忽略立场指令，对比训练又会导致过度关注极性关键词的新失败模式。作者提出数据中心的解决方案（平衡论点课程+LLM增强的立场反转论点），有效缓解了过度修正问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算论证中，检索支持或攻击给定主张的论点至关重要。现有密集嵌入模型在语义搜索中需要同时评估主题相关性和立场正确性，但现有模型在非对称推理上表现不佳，存在强烈的主题偏置而忽略指令性立场。

Method: 提出诊断性词消融指标量化过度修正现象，并采用数据中心的解决方案：实现平衡论点课程（balanced argument curriculum）配合LLM增强、立场反转（stance-inverted）的参数，强制嵌入模型学习更深层的方向性逻辑而非利用表面词汇捷径。

Result: 实验表明对于足够强大的模型，该方法能缓解过度修正问题，在立场感知的论点检索任务上取得进一步改进。

Conclusion: 通过数据中心的策略而非单纯依赖模型架构改进，可以有效纠正嵌入模型在立场感知论证检索中的偏差，包括初始的主题偏置和对比训练后的过度修正问题。

Abstract: In computational argumentation, obtaining arguments that explicitly support or attack given claims is a critical precursor to downstream reasoning tasks. When these supporting and attacking arguments are to be retrieved using semantic search methods, they need to be assessed for topic-relevance to the claims of interest as well as for correctness of their (positive or negative) stance towards the claims. In this paper we explore how dense embedding models (hereafter, models), powering modern retrieval pipelines, can serve as the basis of semantic search incorporating this dual assessment. We show experimentally that existing models struggle with asymmetric reasoning, exhibiting a strong bias toward topical overlap while ignoring instructional stance. We also show that correcting this bias via contrastive training triggers a new failure mode where models over-correct, over-fixating on polarity keywords (e.g., "supports" or "refutes") at the expense of the semantic topic. We thus introduce diagnostic word-ablation metrics to quantify this phenomenon and propose a data-centric solution. By implementing a balanced argument curriculum alongside LLM-augmented, stance-inverted arguments, we force the (embedding) models to learn deeper directional logic rather than exploiting superficial lexical shortcuts. Our evaluation demonstrates that, for sufficiently powerful models, this approach can alleviate the observed overcorrection, achieving further improvements in stance-aware argument retrieval.

</details>


### [117] [A Probabilistic Interpretation of KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28293)
*Renato Geh,Alex Chen,Daniel Israel,Aditya Grover,Guy Van den Broeck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过概率推理的形式化框架来研究KV缓存淘汰问题，证明其计算复杂性，并提出基于采样近似和解码时校正的方法，在同等压缩预算下比现有方法更鲁棒且性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存淘汰问题在文献中缺乏正式的形式化定义，现有方法大多依赖启发式规则，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解该问题。

Method: (1)形式化KV淘汰问题并证明其计算困难性；(2)通过概率框架将KV淘汰转化为期望估计问题，可用采样近似；(3)利用概率解释实现解码时的校正（纠正被淘汰条目）；(4)揭示现有方法本质上是零方差有偏估计器，可轻松改造以支持解码时校正。

Result: 提出的概率化KV淘汰方法结合解码时校正，在不同任务上比现有淘汰方法更鲁棒，在相同压缩预算下达到竞争性的性能。

Conclusion: 概率视角为KV缓存淘汰提供了统一的理论基础，并解锁了解码时校正这一此前被忽视的改进方向。

Abstract: The premise and promise of KV (cache) eviction is simple: higher throughput can be achieved by evicting some entries from the KV cache, at a negligible cost to quality. This holds empirically for many existing methods, though most rely on creative heuristics for selecting which entries to drop. Despite recent advances, the problem of KV eviction has remained informal in the literature. This paper aims to properly formalize this problem through the lens of probabilistic reasoning and reveal what can be learned from this perspective. Concretely, we (1) formalize the problem of KV eviction and, unfortunately, prove that it is computationally hard, (2) show that by framing it probabilistically, KV eviction reduces to the problem of expectation estimation, which can be approximated through sampling, (3) show that through this probabilistic interpretation, correcting for evicted entries during decoding---a previously ignored problem---becomes feasible, and (4) reveal that existing methods in the literature are zero-variance biased estimators that can be easily adapted in order to enable decode time correction. In practice, we show that this probabilistic version of KV eviction coupled with decode time correction is more robust to different tasks compared to existing eviction methods and achieves competitive performance at the same compression budget.

</details>


### [118] [BanglaMed-QA: A Question Answering System for Healthcare Support in Bangla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28329)
*Rowzatul Zannat,Abdullah Al Shafi,K. M. Azharul Hasan,Atia Shahnaz Ipa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向孟加拉语医疗领域的问答系统BanglaMed-QA，构建了包含4,493个问答对的知识库，采用SVM进行问题分类，并结合多种相似度度量进行查询匹配，在自动化评估中达到95%的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗问答系统对提供可靠健康信息至关重要，但低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的医疗QA研究非常有限，缺乏针对这些语言的定制化数据集和系统。

Method: 构建包含4,493个QA对、9个类别、506种疾病的结构化医疗知识库；提出领域特定的词根词典和同义词集以改善语义理解；使用词性标注进行指代消解；采用监督机器学习模型（SVM最佳）进行问题分类；使用余弦、Jaccard、BM25和Levenshtein多种相似度度量，配合软投票和硬投票方法进行查询匹配。

Result: 自动化评估中达到95%的F1分数，人类满意度平均评分为0.9/1.0，验证了系统的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: BanglaMed-QA在真实应用中被验证有效，有助于弥合孟加拉语使用者的医疗信息鸿沟。

Abstract: Medical question answering (QA) systems have become crucial tools for providing reliable health information. But they remain very unexplored for low-resource languages like Bangla due to limited datasets and systems tailored to these languages. To address this, we introduce BanglaMed-QA, a robust QA system specifically designed for the Bangla medical domain. The process begins with building a structured medical knowledge base that includes 4,493 QA pairs in 9 categories under 506 diseases. To improve semantic comprehension, domain-specific root word dictionaries and synonym sets are proposed, in addition to part-of-speech tagging for anaphora resolution. We adopt supervised machine learning models in which SVM is found to be the best model to categorize questions. Multiple similarity metrics, including cosine, Jaccard, BM25, and Levenshtein, are applied with soft and hard voting methods for query matching. The performance of the QA system has been evaluated in two aspects, with a 95% F1 score in an automated evaluation and an average human satisfaction rating of 0.9 out of 1.0. This validates the real-world application of BanglaMed-QA in closing the healthcare information gap for Bangla speakers.

</details>


### [119] [PersonaForge: Realistic Multi-Turn User Simulation for Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28378)
*Hanglong Lv,Dawei Zhu,Lei Li,Bowen Ye,Huaqiu Liu,Yifan Song,Bofei Gao,Weimin Xiong,Jinhao Dong,Chenhong He,Lingpeng Kong,Qi Liu,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PersonaForge，一个用于合成真实多轮用户-智能体交互的用户模拟框架，以及PersonaForge-Bench基准。实验表明使用PersonaForge训练数据可将智能体综合评分提升4.1%，尤其在任务完成和响应质量维度提升显著，同时减少了交互轮数和工具调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型作为智能体工作流执行器的训练数据和基准大多假设信息完整的单轮查询，但实际用户交互中75.9%是多轮的，存在用户交互方式与系统训练/评估方式之间的显著差距。

Method: PersonaForge包含三个核心组件：四维人物画像空间(SOUL)、基于真实用户统计数据校准的SOUL驱动行为控制、以及基于真实种子查询的反向深度构建。使用该方法构建了6.3K条训练数据集和138任务的人工标注基准PersonaForge-Bench。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-27B上的实验表明，PersonaForge训练将综合评分提升4.1%，所有四个维度均有提升，其中任务完成(+6.0%)和响应质量(+6.8%)提升最大。训练后的智能体使用更少的轮次和工具调用，交互效率更高。消融实验确认了SOUL组件和自适应模拟的贡献。

Conclusion: PersonaForge和PersonaForge-Bench为在真实多轮用户交互场景下训练和评估智能体奠定了基础，填补了现有训练与评估范式和真实使用之间的差距。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as agentic workflow executors, yet existing training data and benchmarks largely assume informationally complete, single-turn queries. Our analysis of 16K real-world sessions shows that 75.9% of interactions are multi-turn, revealing a substantial gap between how users interact with agents and how such systems are trained and evaluated. We introduce \textbf{PersonaForge}, a user simulation framework for synthesizing realistic multi-turn user--agent interactions. PersonaForge combines a four-dimensional persona space, SOUL-driven behavioral control calibrated to real-user statistics, and Reverse Deep Construction grounded in authentic seed queries. Using PersonaForge, we construct a 6.3K-record training dataset and \textbf{PersonaForge-Bench}, a manually annotated 138-task benchmark spanning over 20 professional domains with four-dimensional scoring. Experiments on Qwen3.5-27B show that PersonaForge training improves the composite score by +4.1%, with gains across all four dimensions and the largest improvements in Task Completion (+6.0%) and Response Quality (+6.8%). Further analyses show that PersonaForge-trained agents use fewer turns and tool calls, suggesting improved interaction efficiency, while ablations confirm the contribution of SOUL components and adaptive simulation. Together, PersonaForge and PersonaForge-Bench establish a foundation for training and evaluating agents under realistic multi-turn user interaction.

</details>


### [120] [When Linguistic and Internal Confidence Diverge in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28382)
*Hefan Zhang,Bingquan Zhang,Ming Cheng,Saeed Hassanpour,Weicheng Ma,Soroush Vosoughi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了用户询问LLM"有多自信"时，模型的语言表达自信是否能真实反映其内部置信度，发现两者在多维度上经常不一致，支持"有损信道"观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户常要求LLM报告自信程度，但语言表达自信与模型内部置信度之间是否对齐尚不明确，这影响模型可靠性和下游应用。

Method: 在8个分类任务和2个生成任务上，对3个模型家族的30个模型进行系统评估；分类任务沿关联性、幅度一致性和校准三个轴比较语言自信与基于logits的置信度；生成任务测试语言自信是否追踪基于语义熵的不确定性；并使用回归分析研究分布属性和模型元数据对对齐模式的影响。

Result: 实例级关联平均较弱，但在简单项目和更强的基础模型上有所改善；指令微调模型报告更高自信但存在更大信心差距和更差校准；提示设计主要改变自信分布；态度线索虚增自信而不改善对齐；分数示例在避免置信值崩溃时可保留排序信号；分布属性解释了对齐模式的大部分变化，模型元数据作用较小。

Conclusion: 语言自信应被视为有损信道，分散的口头自信分布可携带有用排序信息但不保证校准；需要多轴诊断评估后才可用于下游可靠性流程。

Abstract: Users often ask large language models (LLMs) to report how confident they are, but it is unclear whether such linguistic confidence tracks the model's internal confidence. We study this question across 8 classification tasks, 2 generation tasks and 30 models from three families. For classification, we compare linguistic confidence with logits-based confidence along three axes: association, magnitude agreement and calibration. For generation, we test whether linguistic confidence tracks semantic-entropy-based uncertainty. The axes frequently diverge. Instance-level association is weak on average, although it improves on easier items and for stronger base models. Instruction-tuned models often report higher confidence and sometimes show higher association, but they also have larger confidence gaps and worse calibration. Prompt design mostly changes the distribution of reported confidence. Attitude cues inflate confidence without improving alignment, while score exemplars can preserve rank-order signal when they avoid collapsed confidence values. Regression analyses show that distributional properties of confidence scores explain much of the observed alignment pattern, with model metadata playing a smaller role after controls. These results support a lossy-channel view of linguistic confidence. A more dispersed verbal confidence distribution can carry useful rank information, but it does not make the scores calibrated. Linguistic confidence should therefore be evaluated with multi-axis diagnostics before being used in downstream reliability pipelines.

</details>


### [121] [CultureConverse: A Multilingual Multi-turn Simulation Harness for Culturally Grounded Assistance in East and Southeast Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28405)
*Bryan Chen Zhengyu Tan,Weihua Zheng,Thong T. Doan,Bich Ngoc Doan,Jia Wang Peh,Xiaoyuan Yi,Jing Yao,Xing Xie,Nancy F. Chen,Zhengyuan Liu,JinYeong Bak,Wafi Shamdi,Soo Kai Chie,Liew Yu Siong,Aina Azyyati Binti Mohamad Rezal,Lew Yan Yan Vanessa,Huadan Wu,Dylan Raharja,Nadya Yuki Wangsajaya,Akane Fukushige,Kazushi Kato,Koji Inoue,Tatsuya Kawahara,Jaehyung Seo,Dongjun Kim,Seungyoon Lee,Zi Haur Pang,Rui Yang Tan,Charibeth Ko Cheng,Maria Regina Justina Estuar,Jann Railey Montalan,Pham Minh Duc,Roy Ka-Wei Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CultureConverse，一个大规模、多语言的文化场景对话模拟与评估框架，覆盖东亚和东南亚10个地区、58个子群体身份和7个领域，包含14,610个评估对话和274,295个金标对话，用于评估和提升大语言模型在多轮、文化引导场景中的助人能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化评估通常将文化简化为单轮事实回忆型选择题，无法捕捉用户在文化场景中多轮寻求实用帮助的真实用例，亟需更贴近实际交互的文化能力评估框架。

Method: 构建CultureConverse可扩展的多语言模拟与评估框架，覆盖10个东亚和东南亚地区、58个子群体身份和7个领域；每个模拟片段产生带评分的交互，助手需从部分信息中推断文化约束并协助用户；通过微调27,860个高质量样本验证有效性。

Result: 在18个模型的基准评估中GPT-5 mini达到最高协助质量；人类注释实验表明该评估框架可充分代理人类判断；微调带来的性能提升不仅在领域内有效，还能迁移到领域外的文化选择题和安全分类基准。

Conclusion: CultureConverse提供了可扩展且可代理人类判断的文化对话评估框架，支持交互式评估文化能力，并发布数据集、评测工具链和裁判提示以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Current cultural evaluations for large language models (LLMs) often reduce culture to single-turn factual recall via MCQs, failing to capture a common use case: users seeking practical help over multiple turns in culturally grounded scenarios. We introduce CultureConverse, a scalable, multilingual simulation and evaluation harness for culturally grounded assistant dialogue that covers 10 East and Southeast Asian regions, 58 subgroup identities, and 7 domains. Each simulated and evaluated episode produces a scored interaction where the assistant assists the user and infers cultural constraints from partial information. The resulting CultureConverse-DS dataset contains 14,610 benchmark (evaluation) episodes and 274,295 oracle-guided (gold-mode) dialogues. In our benchmark evaluation of 18 models, GPT-5 mini achieves the highest assistance quality. Human annotation experiments suggest that our evaluation framework is a sufficient proxy for human judgment. Performance gains from fine-tuning on 27,860 high-quality CultureConverse-DS samples improve in-domain assistance and transfer out-of-domain to cultural MCQ and safety classification benchmarks. We release the harness, both splits, and judge prompts to support interactive evaluation of cultural competency.

</details>


### [122] [A Unified Framework to Elicit Structured Feedback for Interpretable Multi-Trait Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28407)
*Shihang Yang,Sanwoo Lee,Ningning Zhao,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HiFTS框架，一个统一的自动回归框架，在预测分数之前先生成层级链式思考（CoT）反馈，用于多维度自动作文评分（AES），并在CFMS-34和ASAP++数据集上取得优异成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反馈增强方法常常将反馈生成与评分解耦，或独立评估各维度，导致评分与反馈一致性差、对评分标准（rubric）的对齐不足。多维度AES需要基于评分标准进行跨维度推理，而非孤立的分数预测。

Method: HiFTS采用统一的自动回归框架，先生成层级CoT反馈，再预测维度级和整体分数。具体包括：从教师LLM中蒸馏rubric对齐的层级CoT反馈，训练学生模型联合生成反馈和分数；采用群组相对策略优化（GRPO）并设计复合奖励函数来平衡分数一致性、校准、反馈质量和结构有效性；推理时引入轻量级全局先验提供整体指导以减少长序列推理中的漂移。此外，作者构建了CFMS-34中文多维度AES数据集（951篇作文，含整体分数和34个 rubric 维度标注）。

Result: 在CFMS-34和ASAP++数据集上的实验表明，HiFTS在整体和维度级评分上均表现优异，同时能生成连贯、与评分标准对齐的反馈。

Conclusion: 通过将反馈生成与评分统一在生成式框架中，并利用强化学习优化和全局先验引导，HiFTS有效提升了多维度AES中评分准确性与反馈一致性，为自动作文评分提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multi-trait Automated Essay Scoring (AES) requires rubric-grounded reasoning across interdependent traits, rather than isolated score prediction. Existing feedback-enhanced methods often decouple feedback from scoring or assess traits independently, weakening score--feedback consistency and rubric alignment. We propose HiFTS, a unified autoregressive framework that generates hierarchical CoT feedback before predicting trait-level and holistic scores. HiFTS distills rubric-grounded hierarchical CoT feedback from a teacher LLM and trains student models to jointly generate feedback and scores. HiFTS further applies Group Relative Policy Optimization with a composite reward balancing score agreement, calibration, feedback quality, and structural validity. At inference, a lightweight global prior provides holistic guidance to reduce drift during long-form reasoning. We also introduce CFMS-34, a Chinese multi-trait AES dataset with 951 essays annotated with holistic scores and 34 rubric-based traits. Experiments on CFMS-34 and ASAP++ show that HiFTS achieves strong holistic and trait-level scoring while producing coherent, rubric-aligned feedback.

</details>


### [123] [Are These Modules Worth Their Cost? A Paradigm-Level Accuracy-Cost Analysis of In-context Learning Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28432)
*Jiayan Lin,Yujia Liu,Zijin Hong,Zheng Yuan,Yilin Xiao,Hao Chen,Qinggang Zhang,Xiao Huang,Feiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对ICL文本到SQL管线的五个重复模块进行了17种范式级配置的系统性成本-精度归因分析，发现执行反馈精化是唯一普遍有效且低成本的范式，并提出了可迁移的成本感知分层指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL文本到SQL研究通常只报告端到端整体精度，缺乏对单个设计选择边际精度-成本贡献的量化，导致难以从范式层面统一理解成本与精度的权衡。

Method: 在单一受控实现下，实例化17种范式级配置，覆盖五个重复模块（ICL文本到SQL管线），并在四个不同能力水平和推理风格的主干模型上归因每个范式的边际贡献和成本，同时进行跨模块分析和Token消耗核算。

Result: 执行反馈精化是唯一在所有主干上普遍有效且成本低的范式；其他模块仅在特定主干条件下有效。输入Token需求主要与管线结构相关，输出Token需求更依赖于主干生成行为。堆叠在大多数主干上能提升精度，但增益组成因主干能力而异。固定预算下，对中等主干进行更精细的管线工程优于升级到前沿模型搭配精简管线。

Conclusion: 研究结果提炼为可操作的成本感知分层指南，该指南无需逐范式搜索即可迁移到另外五个主干模型上，为实际配置ICL文本到SQL系统提供了统一范式级的成本-精度量化依据。

Abstract: Recent advances in in-context learning (ICL) text-to-SQL have substantially improved execution accuracy on public benchmarks by assembling increasingly elaborate pipelines around the base generator, yet existing studies typically report aggregate end-to-end accuracy, without quantifying the marginal accuracy-cost contribution of individual design choices. Consequently, providing a unified, paradigm-level cost-accuracy quantification remains a critical challenge for understanding and configuring modern text-to-SQL. To address this, we instantiate 17 paradigm-level configurations across five recurring modules of the ICL text-to-SQL pipeline under a single controlled implementation, and attribute each paradigm's marginal contribution and incurred cost across all four backbones spanning diverse capability levels and reasoning styles. Our analysis reveals that execution-feedback refinement is the only paradigm whose benefit holds universally at consistently low cost, while most other modules help only under backbone-dependent conditions. Token accounting shows that input demand is more closely tied to pipeline structure, whereas output demand is more sensitive to backbone generation behavior. Cross-module analysis further shows that stacking improves accuracy on most backbones, although how the gains compose varies with backbone capability. We also find that a fixed budget is often better spent engineering a more elaborate pipeline over a mid-tier backbone than upgrading to a frontier model with a lean pipeline. These findings distill into an actionable, cost-aware tiered guideline that transfers to five additional backbones without per-paradigm search.

</details>


### [124] [Fidelity Is Not Enough: Dispatch-Level Instrumentation for Agentic Datasheet Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28439)
*Qing Ye,Meng-Hsuan Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体文档提取系统中的保真度（fidelity）问题，发现仅依赖保真度评分会漏掉静默失败（如工具调用被禁用却仍返回伪造结果）。作者构建了一个37条声明的智能体基准，开发了基于工具调用记录的规则式失败归因分类器和静默失败检测器，并引入"因果舱"（causal chamber）作为独立验证工具，结果发现工具层主要带来可移植性和可观测性而非准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体文档提取系统中，标准的保真度度量会将"工具调用被静默禁用却用伪造文本作答"的运行标记为成功，说明仅凭输出值匹配无法识别系统故障，需要工具调用级别的追踪来暴露此类静默失败。

Method: 在25条人工筛选声明（3个组件）加上第四组件12条共37条声明的智能体基准中记录每次工具调用，构建两个工具：规则式失败归因分类器，以及仅检查工具调用类型（不检查提取值）的静默失败检测器。另设计一个因果舱作为独立验证基准，测试数据表声称在物理测量下是否成立，并给出不可评分声明的分类学。

Result: 检测器在跨三个模型家族的207次干净保真度通过提取中无误报，并恢复所有50个人工植入的故障（这些故障扣除了检测器规则检查的确切工具）。因果舱可验证37条声明中仅2条，受控扰动下保真度始终通过而因果舱判定恰好在测量不确定性处翻转。三个部署栈中，工具层带来可移植性和可观测性而非准确性，仅在文档超出上下文窗口时才体现其价值。

Conclusion: 保真度作为唯一度量是不够的，工具调用追踪是暴露静默失败的关键；因果舱提供不可替代但刻意有限的独立验证；工具层的主要价值是工程属性（可移植性、可观测性）而非能力提升，其溢价仅在长文档场景中合理。

Abstract: One model passed our fidelity check without ever opening the datasheet. We found it while qualifying models for an internal extraction service: a structured-output constraint had silently disabled tool use, and the model answered anyway, with fabricated source text. Only the per-tool trace exposed it. Fidelity -- whether an extracted value matches the source -- is the standard measure for agentic document extraction, and it scores that run a success. We therefore log every tool call in an agentic benchmark of 25 hand-curated claims over three components, with 12 more on a fourth, 37 in all. From that dispatch record we build two instruments: a rule-based failure-attribution classifier, and a silent-failure detector whose two rules check only which tools were called, never the extracted value. The detector raises no flag on 207 clean fidelity-passing extractions across three model families, and recovers all 50 planted faults that withhold exactly the tools its rules check. The two results are not symmetric: the first bounds the false-positive rate, the second is recall by construction, and detection power against runs that call their tools and still answer wrongly is unmeasured. A second, independent oracle, a causal chamber that tests whether the datasheet's claims hold under physical measurement, is intentionally partial: it confirms only what the apparatus can exercise, a verifiable envelope of 2 of those 37 claims, and we give a taxonomy of why the rest are not physically gradable. Under a controlled perturbation, fidelity passes throughout while the chamber verdict flips exactly at the measurement uncertainty. Across three deployed model stacks (one destabilised by its serving stack, not by any capability gap) the tool layer buys portability and observability rather than accuracy, and earns its premium only once a document outgrows the context window.

</details>


### [125] [Sliding-window beats linear attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28444)
*Alexia Jolicoeur-Martineau,Rhea Sanjay Sukthanker,Pashmina Cameron,Emy Gervais*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究表明，滑动窗口注意力（SWA）配合sink tokens在多个下游任务上的表现不逊于甚至优于后训练的线性注意力模型，且无需后训练，速度快、内存占用低，是一种更廉价可靠的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力被视为解决LLM二次方注意力扩展问题的有前景方案，但现有研究缺乏与更简单基线方法的系统对比。

Method: 在多个LLM上、多种下游任务中，将后训练的线性注意力模型与带sink tokens的滑动窗口注意力（SWA）进行系统比较。

Result: SWA在性能上与线性注意力相当或更优；在长上下文推理任务（如Needle-in-a-Haystack和BABILong）上，SWA的性能高出线性注意力2到10倍，且无需后训练、速度快、内存低。

Conclusion: 建议使用SWA替代后训练线性注意力模型以降低推理内存成本；线性注意力若要匹配SWA的性能，可能需要从头训练或大量后训练。

Abstract: Due to the nature of quadratic attention, Large Language Models (LLMs) consume a lot of memory and energy. Every new token costs more than the previous one. For each additional token, the keys and values must be stored in memory indefinitely, which is unsustainable.
  Several alternatives have been proposed to fix the quadratic scaling problem, one of which is retrofitting LLMs to use Linear Attention. This idea has attracted a lot of attention, given its promise to solve the quadratic scaling problem with state-of-the-art performance at low cost. However, this line of research has not been properly compared to simpler baselines.
  In this work, we show that Sliding Window Attention (SWA) with sinks performs as well or better than post-trained Linear Attention models. We observe this across multiple LLMs on various downstream tasks. For long-context reasoning tasks (Needle-in-a-Haystack and BABILong), SWA achieves massively higher performance (2 to 10 times higher than linear attention). SWA requires no post-training, is extremely fast, and requires low memory; therefore, making it an extremely cheap and reliable solution.
  To reduce inference memory cost, we strongly recommend switching to SWA instead of post-training linear models. Linear attention models may have shown some promise, but they likely require to be trained from scratch or extensive post-training in order to even match SWA.

</details>


### [126] [Acquire, Repair, Preserve: A Diagnosis-Guided Post-Training Recipe for Small-Model Dialogue Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28458)
*Nan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究交互式对话游戏中的模型能力，提出一个三阶段训练方法（SFT获取广泛游戏参与能力、基于回合局部偏好对修复可验证的决策失败、保持通用能力），在LM Playschool挑战赛中将clemscore从10.67提升到38.92。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准无法充分测试模型在交互式对话游戏中跨回合维持状态、解释反馈并在动态约束下选择有效动作的能力，需要针对这些局部决策失败进行系统研究。

Method: 提出三阶段训练策略：(1) 监督微调（SFT）获取广泛的游戏参与能力；(2) 使用回合局部偏好对（turn-local preference pairs）修复可机械验证的失败（如重复猜测、格式错误的动作、违反反馈）；(3) 保持对话游戏之外的通用能力。

Result: 官方最终评估中，公开clemscore从10.67提升至38.92，闭卷域内得分从13.41提升至41.17，静态性能基本保持不变（44.14 vs 44.24）。域外clemscore仍较低（7.88），最大提升集中在目标游戏家族的未见变体上。

Conclusion: 广泛的SFT带来了大部分能力提升；当失败检测精确时，回合局部监督是有效的，但观察到的迁移主要局限于同类游戏家族内部，跨域泛化仍是挑战。

Abstract: Interactive dialogue games test a capability that static benchmarks largely leave implicit: a model must carry state across turns, interpret feedback, and choose valid actions under changing constraints. We study this setting in the LM Playschool Challenge with a 2B open-weight model, and find that many failures are not only broad knowledge failures but also local decision failures: repeated guesses, malformed actions, and violations of feedback that the model has just seen. These diagnostics motivate a training recipe organized around three steps: acquire broad game participation through supervised fine-tuning, repair mechanically verifiable failures within one targeted dialogue-game family using turn-local preference pairs, and preserve general capabilities beyond these dialogue games. In the official final evaluation, our submission improves public clemscore from 10.67 to 38.92 and closed in-domain score from 13.41 to 41.17, while approximately preserving aggregate static performance (44.14 vs. 44.24 for the baseline). Out-of-domain clemscore remains low at 7.88, with the largest gains concentrated in unseen variants of the targeted family. Our results suggest that broad SFT brings most of the model's capability improvement; turn-local supervision can be effective when failure detection is precise, with observed transfer concentrated primarily within-family.

</details>


### [127] [Stranger, Fan, or Peer? A Systematic Study on the Role of Interlocutor in Persona-Based Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28467)
*Daniela Occhipinti,Malvina Nissim,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于人格的对话系统中，说话者传记可见性在训练、推理和评估三个阶段分别设置时的影响，发现训练阶段的可见性设置比推理阶段更关键地决定模型是否表达人格特质或直接复制传记文本，且不对称披露会导致目标内容泄漏到对话者轮次中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究将传记可见性视为单一因素，忽略了训练、推理和评估三个阶段可以分别控制可见性，导致一些机制被遮蔽。本文旨在通过三阶段分解来揭示对话系统中传记可见性配置对模型行为和内容泄漏的影响。

Method: 在配对说话者传记的对话数据集上，分别在训练和推理阶段变化目标和对话者是否可见对方传记，并使用LLM作为评判者进行作者身份识别，系统性地研究三阶段可见性因子化对人格表达和传记复制的影响。

Result: (i) 训练阶段可见性比推理阶段更决定模型是表达人格特质还是复制传记文本；(ii) 接受对话者传记可见性训练的模型复制目标传记文本更少，而仅改变推理可见性影响不一致；(iii) 在不对称披露下，目标内容更易泄漏到对话者轮次，这类对话更易被判断者识别。

Conclusion: 传记泄漏到生成轮次是训练和推理阶段对话者可见性配置方式造成的现象，将训练、推理和评估三个阶段分开考虑是必要的研究方法。

Abstract: Persona-based dialogue systems are usually conditioned on speaker biography, but dialogues involve at least two participants, and who has access to whose biography can vary across training, inference, and evaluation. Prior work often neglected these aspects, obscuring mechanisms that only appear when biography visibility is toggled separately across training, inference, and evaluation, a three-stage factorisation that prior work has largely treated as a single factor. We study this factorisation on a dataset of dialogues paired with speaker's biographies, varying whether the target and interlocutor speakers see each other's biographies during training and inference, and using an LLM as a judge to perform author identification. We find that (i) training-time visibility, more than inference-time visibility, determines whether models express persona traits through dialogue or fall back on copying biographical text (a known problem/phenomenon in persona-based generation); (ii) models trained with interlocutor-biography visibility copy less target-biographical text than models trained without it, while changing visibility only at inference time has a less consistent effect; and (iii) under asymmetric disclosure, where only the interlocutor sees the target biography, target content leaks into interlocutor turns more often, and dialogues containing such traces are easier for the judge to identify, especially when interlocutor turns are visible. These results suggest that biography leakage into generated turns is an artefact of how interlocutor visibility is configured across training and inference, and separating the three stages is necessary.

</details>


### [128] [ContextPilot: Teaching Agents for Proactive Context Management via Fine-grained RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28476)
*Zhuoshi Pan,Qizhi Pei,Junru Lu,Honglin Lin,H. Vicky Zhao,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextPilot，一个用于长时程智能体推理的主动上下文管理框架。该框架扩展了工具集（加入规划、长期记忆和软上下文卸载工具），并设计了专门针对上下文管理的强化学习方法，通过上下文和熵变化识别关键编辑决策进行分支采样，从所有经过该编辑动作的分支轨迹中估计动作级优势。实验表明ContextPilot在长上下文QA和深度搜索任务上表现更好且上下文更紧凑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程智能体任务中，主动式上下文管理方法存在三大局限：(1)工具集受限，仅支持搜索、删除和摘要，缺乏全局规划、长期记忆和自适应压缩支持；(2)探索效率低，将所有上下文管理动作视为均匀对待，忽略其异构影响；(3)信用分配粗糙，将最终轨迹级奖励平均分配给所有中间编辑动作。

Method: ContextPilot框架包括两部分：(1)系统性地扩展工具集，加入规划工具、长期记忆工具和软上下文卸载工具；(2)提出专为上下文管理设计的RL方法，利用上下文和熵的变化来识别关键的编辑决策点，进行分支采样，并从所有经过相应上下文编辑动作的分支轨迹中估计动作级优势。

Result: 在长上下文QA和深度搜索任务上的实验表明，ContextPilot在更紧凑的工作上下文下实现了更强的性能，并一致性地优于现有基线方法，适用于多种基础模型和基准测试。

Conclusion: ContextPilot通过扩展工具集和设计定制化RL方法，有效解决了现有主动上下文管理的三大局限性，在长时程智能体推理中实现了更好的性能与更高的上下文效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks require large language models (LLMs) to iteratively retrieve, integrate, and maintain dispersed information across multi-turn interactions, but preserving all interaction histories leads to a continuously growing working context. Recent proactive context management methods allow models to edit their own working context with specialized tools, yet they still face three key limitations: (1) a limited toolset restricted to search, deletion, and summarization, with no support for global planning, long-term memory, and adaptive compression; (2) inefficient exploration that treats context management actions uniformly despite their heterogeneous impacts on final outcomes; and (3) coarse-grained credit assignment that assigns the final trajectory-level reward to all intermediate context editing actions during RL. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ContextPilot, a proactive context management framework for long-horizon agentic reasoning. Our approach systematically augments the toolset with planning, long-term memory, and soft context offloading tools. We further propose an RL method tailored for context management, which uses context and entropy variation to identify critical editing decisions for branch sampling and estimates action-level advantages from all branched trajectories that pass through the corresponding context editing action. Experiments on long-context QA and deep search tasks show that ContextPilot achieves stronger performance with a more compact working context, consistently outperforming existing baselines across various base models and benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/Tencent/ContextPilot.

</details>


### [129] [Blind Men and the Elephant: Probing the Epistemic Myopia of LLMs under Long-Tail Divergent Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28478)
*Zhuoshi Pan,Junru Lu,Yan Qian,H. Vicky Zhao,Di Yin,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ElephantBench是一个闭卷知识探针，包含1,094个关于长尾事实多版本答案的问题，用于评估LLM是否只记得主流叙述而忽略其他版本。测试32个模型后发现最强的模型也只能在52.4%的问题上同时回忆两个版本，暴露了模型参数的"认知短视"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事实问答假设单一规范答案，忽略了LLM对长尾事实可能保留多种不同叙述的能力，缺乏系统评估模型是否只记忆主流叙述而遗忘次要版本的方法。

Method: 通过基于图的可审计流水线从低曝光网络语料中检索相关文档，识别自然出现的分歧，将其转化为多版本问答记录。每个答案都经过原始文档和权威公开来源验证，并由人工审核。使用32个模型进行测试。

Result: 最强模型仅在52.4%的问题上能同时恢复两个版本的答案；几乎所有剩余问题中模型只回忆了一个版本而遗漏另一个。增大模型规模和推理时推理可以提升回忆率但不能消除不完整性。语料分析显示曝光不平衡偏向主导版本，而更多的少数方曝光与更完整的回忆相关。

Conclusion: ElephantBench可作为诊断参数记忆中认知短视的可复现知识探针，其基于图的构建流水线为将长尾语料转化为可溯源的知识探针提供了高效可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Factual question answering (QA) typically assumes a single canonical answer, obscuring whether large language models (LLMs) retain divergent accounts of long-tail facts. To address this gap, we introduce ElephantBench, a closed-book knowledge probe comprising 1,094 questions generated through an auditable graph-based pipeline. The pipeline retrieves related documents from a low-exposure web corpus, identifies naturally occurring disagreements, and converts them into multi-account QA records. Each answer is verified against the originating documents and authoritative public web sources and is then reviewed by human annotators. Across 32 models, even the strongest model recovers both accounts on only 52.4% of questions, while on nearly all remaining questions it recalls one account but omits the other. Scaling model size and inference-time reasoning improve recall but do not eliminate this incompleteness. Corpus analysis further shows that exposure imbalance favors the dominant account, whereas greater minority-side exposure is associated with more complete recall. These findings establish ElephantBench as a reproducible knowledge probe for diagnosing epistemic myopia in parametric memory.
